### [1] [A Cone-Constrained Bilinear Decomposition for Total Scaled-Gradient Variation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00036)
*Haibin Su,Chunlin Wu,Huibin Chang,Zhifang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对TSGV正则项的双线性分解与交替最小化求解框架，在保持边缘和角点的同时改善非凸非线性优化的可计算性与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSGV正则项能有效刻画分片线性图像结构并保留边缘、角点，但其高度非凸、非线性特征导致现有算法对参数敏感、计算困难且缺乏可靠收敛保证。

Method: 作者设计双线性分解以解耦TSGV中的非线性加权梯度，将原问题等价改写为带锥约束或球约束的优化问题；采用结合majorization-minimization策略的交替最小化方法求解，并在Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz框架下证明全局收敛到临界点。

Result: 在高斯去噪和NLOS成像实验中，该方法在PSNR、SSIM上达到或超过代表性变分方法，尤其在高噪声水平下表现更优，并在稠密与稀疏扫描条件下提升结构重建质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为TSGV正则化提供了具有几何解释、无需步长调参且具备收敛保证的有效优化框架，适用于边缘与角点保持型图像恢复任务。

Abstract: The total scaled-gradient variation (TSGV) regularizer, derived from sparse modeling of piecewise-linear structures, has been shown to preserve edges and corners in image restoration. However, its highly nonconvex and nonlinear nature poses severe computational challenges, as existing methods often suffer from parameter sensitivity or lack convergence guarantees. To overcome this, we propose a tailored bilinear decomposition that decouples the nonlinear weighted gradient in the TSGV regularizer. This approach yields an equivalent optimization problem governed by cone or sphere constraints, depending on the chosen scaling function. In particular, the cone constraint plays a central role in characterizing edge- and corner-preserving behavior. We solve this reformulation using the alternating minimization method (AMM) equipped with a majorization--minimization strategy, ensuring a monotonic decrease in energy without step-size tuning. Furthermore, we provide a geometric interpretation of the edge-preserving properties of these constraints by analyzing their asymptotic behavior near image singularities. We establish the global convergence of the proposed method to a critical point within the Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz framework. Extensive numerical experiments on Gaussian denoising and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) imaging show that the proposed method achieves PSNR and SSIM competitive with or superior to representative variational methods, especially at high noise levels, and improves the structural reconstruction under dense and sparse scanning.

</details>


### [2] [Qwen-Drive-1.0: An Initial Step towards a Vision-Language Foundation Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00111)
*Xin Zhou,Zongchuang Zhao,Zhibo Yang,Mingsheng Li,Humen Zhong,Shuai Bai,Du Chu,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Jun Tang,Qiuyue Wang,Mingkun Yang,Jiazhao Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen-Drive-1.0 是一个面向自动驾驶的视觉-语言基础模型，将 3D 感知、驾驶场景问答与运动规划统一到预训练 VLM 框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统通常将感知、理解和规划分为多个模块，难以充分利用通用视觉-语言模型的表示能力与指令理解能力。该工作希望在保留通用 VLM 能力的同时，赋予模型自动驾驶所需的 3D 场景理解和轨迹规划能力。

Method: 模型保留预训练 VLM 架构，并引入外部 BEV 感知头，用于联合完成 3D 目标检测、语义占据预测和 BEV 地图分割；同时设计 Planning Expert，基于共享 VLM 表征生成未来自车轨迹。训练上采用分阶段策略，将驾驶监督数据与通用视觉-语言数据结合，以平衡驾驶专用能力与通用视觉语言能力。

Result: 实验显示，该模型在 3D 感知、驾驶场景理解方面表现较强，同时基本保留通用视觉-语言能力；在 open-loop、pseudo-closed-loop 和 closed-loop 等多种评估设置下，运动规划性能也具有较强竞争力。

Conclusion: Qwen-Drive-1.0 表明，将预训练 VLM 扩展到自动驾驶任务是可行的，并且统一框架能够同时支持可解释的 3D 场景结构建模、语言交互和轨迹规划，为自动驾驶基础模型提供了有价值的初步探索。

Abstract: We present Qwen-Drive-1.0, an initial step towards a vision-language foundation model for autonomous driving. Qwen-Drive-1.0 retains the architecture of the pretrained vision-language model (VLM) and integrates 3D perception, visual question answering, and motion planning within a unified framework. An external bird's-eye-view (BEV) perception head jointly performs 3D object detection, semantic occupancy prediction, and BEV map segmentation. It serves as a probe of the 3D information accessible from the shared representations and provides an explicit, inspectable interface to 3D scene structure. A Planning Expert conditions on shared VLM representations to generate future ego trajectories. A staged training recipe combines driving supervision with general-purpose vision-language data to acquire driving-specific competence while helping preserve broad visual understanding and instruction-following capabilities. Experiments demonstrate strong 3D perception and driving scene understanding while largely preserving general vision-language capability. Comprehensive evaluations across open-loop, pseudo-closed-loop, and closed-loop settings further show highly competitive motion-planning performance.

</details>


### [3] [ZimaBlue: Evolving Generalizable World Action Models through Scalable Video Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00188)
*Xionghao Wu,Yijun Yang,Shiyang Zhou,Haoze Sun,Jianhui Liu,Songsong Yu,Jiyao Zhang,Wenbo Li,Bo Wang,Guoqing Ma,Lin Song,Renjie Liao,Shenghe Zheng,Wei Tang,Xiaojuan Qi,Yanwei Li,Yuan Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZimaBlue 通过大规模第一视角视频学习可泛化的世界动作模型，将无动作标注的具身视频经验转化为实时机器人控制能力，并显著提升零样本操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作要实现鲁棒泛化需要大量真实物理交互经验，但带动作标注的机器人轨迹采集成本高、规模和多样性受限；相比之下，第一视角视频规模更大，包含丰富的物体交互、接触动态、工具使用和长时程行为，因此需要一种方法把这些无动作视频转化为有效控制策略。

Method: 提出 ZimaBlue 框架，采用三阶段训练流程：首先在大规模人类与机器人第一视角视频上进行因果具身视频预训练；然后通过带统一动作表示的视频-动作中期训练，将视觉动态与异构机器人轨迹对齐；最后针对目标机器人进行专门化部署。为满足实时控制，采用异步 Slow-Fast 双系统架构：Slow 世界模型提供强时空表征，Fast 分支实现 30 Hz 动作预测。

Result: 在真实机器人零样本评估中，从仅使用目标机器人数据扩展到超过 120,000 小时具身视频后，成功率由 36.1% 提升至 77.8%。在多个基准测试上也表现强劲，尤其在未见任务上提升明显。

Conclusion: 大规模无动作具身视频可以作为机器人控制泛化能力的重要数据来源；通过分阶段训练和 Slow-Fast 实时架构，ZimaBlue 能有效学习可泛化的世界动作模型，并在真实机器人任务中获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation faces a fundamental scaling challenge: robust generalization demands broad physical experience, yet action-labeled robot trajectories are expensive to collect and inherently limited in diversity. Egocentric videos offer a far more scalable source of embodied experience, capturing object interactions, contact dynamics, tool use, and long-horizon behaviors across diverse environments. The central challenge is how to convert this abundant but action-free experience into effective robot control. We introduce ZimaBlue, a scalable framework for learning generalizable World Action Models (WAMs) from large-scale video. ZimaBlue follows a three-stage training curriculum: it first performs causal embodied video pre-training on large-scale human and robot egocentric videos, then grounds the learned visual dynamics in heterogeneous robot trajectories through video-action mid-training with a unified action representation, and finally specializes the model to a target robot for deployment. To make generative WAMs practical for real-time control, ZimaBluefurther adopts an asynchronous Slow-Fast dual-system architecture, where a high-capacity Slow world model provides generalizable spatiotemporal representations and a lightweight Fast branch enables 30 Hz action prediction on NVIDIA RTX 4090. On real-robot zero-shot evaluations, scaling from target-robot data alone to over 120,000 hours of embodied video improves success from 36.1% to 77.8%. ZimaBlue further delivers strong performance across multiple benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [4] [A Lagrangian View of Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00198)
*Peyman Milanfar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从拉格朗日粒子视角重新推导显式时间生成模型，说明 Flow Matching 的直线轨迹可由保持目标身份不变的局部条件及相应对流 PDE 解析得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Flow Matching、Rectified Flow 等模型多从最优传输与连续性方程出发，采用欧拉视角描述概率质量的宏观输运。作者希望提供一种更微观、粒子中心的机械解释，以阐明为什么直线流能够支持大步长生成，以及轨迹弯曲的数学来源。

Method: 作者分析连续去噪器的局部 Taylor 展开，提出单步最优生成所需的严格不变性条件，即目标身份守恒。将该条件形式化后得到一个准线性对流偏微分方程，并使用特征线方法求解，从几何角度研究去噪器 Jacobian 对轨迹形状的影响。

Result: 通过特征线方法解析得到 Flow Matching 中的直线轨迹，并指出去噪器的 Jacobian 是导致轨迹曲率的主要因素。该框架解释了直线流为何允许使用很大的采样步长，也解释了经验模型中为何需要蒸馏来压平特征线交叉造成的弯曲或不一致。

Conclusion: 论文将显式时间生成模型的直线流性质从粒子动力学和 PDE 角度进行了统一解释，为理解 Flow Matching 的高效采样、轨迹曲率来源以及蒸馏作用提供了新的几何与解析视角。

Abstract: Modern explicit-time generative models, such as Flow Matching [Lipman et al., 2023] and Rectified Flow [Liu et al., 2023], are typically derived top-down via Optimal Transport and the continuity equation. This standard Eulerian approach focuses on the macroscopic transport of probability mass. In this paper, we present an alternative, bottom-up mechanical derivation grounded in a Lagrangian (particle-centric) perspective. By analyzing the local Taylor expansion of a continuous denoiser, we motivate a strict invariance condition required for optimal, singlestep generation: the conservation of target identity. Enforcing this condition yields a governing quasi-linear advection Partial Differential Equation (PDE). We demonstrate that solving this PDE via the Method of Characteristics analytically yields the straight-line trajectories of Flow Matching. This geometric perspective isolates the Jacobian of the denoiser as the primary source of trajectory curvature, providing a direct mathematical explanation for why straight-line flows enable massive step sizes, and why empirical models require distillation to flatten intersecting characteristics.

</details>


### [5] [Distributed Implicit Harm: A Compositional Safety Blind Spot in MLLM-Based Video Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00206)
*Ruotong Wang,Zihao Zhu,Siwei Lyu,Xin Tao,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统研究视频审核中的“分布式隐性伤害”（DIH）：单个片段或模态看似无害，但组合后形成有害含义。作者构建合成数据集并评测30多个MLLM，发现现有模型普遍难以识别这类组合性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频安全数据与审核模型多依赖局部视觉线索、关键词或单模态信号，难以覆盖由时间片段关系或音视频跨模态关系共同产生的隐性有害语义。由于这类视频难以检索、标注和收集，相关风险长期被低估。

Method: 作者定义DIH及两个代表性子类：时间分布式伤害DIH-T和跨模态伤害DIH-M；提出多智能体合成框架，将单独无害的组件组合成有害场景，并生成带显式推理标注的9000余个视频数据集；随后在30多个闭源前沿模型与开源MLLM上进行基准评测，并补充真实社交媒体DIH视频验证。

Result: 实验显示，现有MLLM在DIH-T和DIH-M检测上存在显著且一致的缺陷。即使最强的前沿模型能够正确判断孤立组件无害，也常常无法识别组件组合后涌现出的有害含义。真实世界视频评测也复现了这一失败模式。

Conclusion: DIH是视频内容审核中一个实际存在但研究不足的组合性安全挑战。论文表明，当前MLLM缺乏对跨时间、跨模态关系语义的安全推理能力，未来需要更强的组合推理、关系建模和DIH导向的数据与评测机制。

Abstract: Despite their growing use in video moderation, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit a compositional safety blind spot: videos composed of seemingly benign components can convey harmful meaning when interpreted as a whole. We refer to this phenomenon as Distributed Implicit Harm (DIH), where harm arises from relations among components distributed along a decomposition axis of the video, rather than from any single explicit cue. Among many possible axes, we study two representative cases: temporally distributed harm across visual segments (DIH-T) and cross-modal harm between audio and visual streams (DIH-M). Studying and mitigating DIH at scale requires data that is difficult to collect: such videos lack compositional harm annotations, evade retrieval based on local visual cues, keywords, or single-modality signals, and are consequently absent from existing safety datasets. To bridge this gap, we develop a multi-agent synthesis framework that composes individually benign components into harmful scenarios and generates diverse DIH videos with explicit reasoning annotations, yielding a dataset of over 9,000 videos spanning visual-only and audio-visual settings. Benchmarking over 30 MLLMs spanning frontier proprietary models and leading open-source systems reveals substantial and consistent deficits in detecting both DIH-T and DIH-M. Notably, this failure persists even among the strongest frontier models: they often correctly assess individual components in isolation but fail to recognize the harmful meaning that emerges from their composition. We further evaluate these models on a manually collected set of real-world DIH videos from social media and observe the same failure mode, highlighting DIH as a practical and underexplored challenge for video moderation.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Language Priors: Diagnosing and Fixing Visual-Origin Hallucinations in Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00231)
*Peiyang Xu,Xiaopei Zhu,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大语言模型的物体幻觉不仅源于语言先验，也源于视觉特征提取错误与图文嵌入错位，并提出ACFT方法以低数据量、零推理开销显著降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多将MLLM中的物体幻觉归因于文本共现统计等语言先验，忽视了视觉端特征提取错误、图文表示不对齐等视觉来源因素。本文旨在量化并诊断这种“视觉源幻觉”，并据此设计有效的缓解方法。

Method: 作者通过余弦相似度分析和Smooth Grad-CAM熵度量比较幻觉样本与正常样本的图文相似性和注意力分布；进一步提出Adversarial Contrastive Fine-Tuning（ACFT），利用Adversarial Hallucination Attribute Flipping（AHAF）生成最小定向扰动，使图像的幻觉属性翻转，构造严格对齐的正负样本对进行对比微调。

Result: 实验显示幻觉样本具有系统性更低的图文相似度，并出现异常注意力模式；ACFT仅使用COCO数据集0.9%，无额外推理开销，在LLaVA、MiniGPT-4、Qwen2.5-VL上于POPE、MME及四个描述级幻觉基准取得领先性能。

Conclusion: 物体幻觉存在重要的视觉来源，MLLM视觉表示接近幻觉决策边界。基于视觉诊断构造对抗式对比微调样本可高效提升图文对齐与抗幻觉能力。

Abstract: Existing research on object hallucination in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) predominantly attributes the problem to language priors such as over-reliance on textual co-occurrence statistics. We challenge this view by presenting quantitative evidence for a complementary, under-explored cause: visual-origin hallucination, where hallucinations arise from incorrect visual feature extraction and misalignment between image and text embeddings. Through cosine similarity analysis and Smooth Grad-CAM entropy measurements, we show that hallucinated samples exhibit systematically lower image-text similarity (average 0.158 vs. -0.122) and inverted attention patterns, where attention is dispersed when the target object is present but wrongly concentrated when it is absent. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose Adversarial Contrastive Fine-Tuning (ACFT). ACFT uses an Adversarial Hallucination Attribute Flipping (AHAF) procedure, involving minimal, targeted adversarial perturbations that flip an image's hallucination attribute, to construct perfectly aligned positive-negative pairs, which are then used for contrastive fine-tuning. AHAF simultaneously serves as a diagnostic probe, revealing that MLLM visual representations lie dangerously close to hallucination decision boundaries. Requiring only 0.9% of the COCO dataset and adding zero inference overhead, ACFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on POPE, MME, and four description-level hallucination benchmarks across LLaVA, MiniGPT-4, and Qwen2.5-VL. Code is available at https://github.com/zxp555/ACFT_MM

</details>


### [7] [Beyond Blind Compliance: Benchmarking Task Verification in OCR Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00232)
*Yue Zhou,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VeriOCRBench，用于评估多模态大语言模型在 OCR 场景中是否能先判断任务是否可执行，而不是盲目作答。评测显示 15 个主流 MLLM 普遍存在盲从、诊断失败和过度拒答问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OCR 文档理解与富文本视觉推理评测通常默认问题都是有效且可回答的，但真实 OCR 场景中常出现文字不可读、证据被遮挡、目标不存在、前提矛盾或变量缺失等情况。因此需要评估模型在回答前验证图像前提、文本前提和问题是否共同构成可执行任务的能力。

Method: 作者定义 OCR-grounded Task Verification 任务，并构建 VeriOCRBench：包含 1,800 个经人工验证的样本，来源于 8 个 OCR 相关数据集，覆盖 8 类真实图像领域。其中 1,600 个为注入陷阱的无效任务，覆盖 8 种陷阱类型和视觉、语境、事实、逻辑四个验证维度；另有 200 个无陷阱控制样本用于衡量过度拒答。数据构建采用 Visual Atomic Fact（VAF）锚定流程并经过完整人工审计。

Result: 在 15 个领先 MLLM 上的评估发现，模型仍存在持续的盲目服从无效任务、无法准确诊断无效根因，以及在提示影响下出现过度拒答等问题。

Conclusion: VeriOCRBench 揭示了当前 OCR 推理系统在任务有效性验证方面的关键可靠性缺口，说明未来 MLLM 不仅需要提升 OCR 和推理能力，还需要具备更稳健的任务可执行性判断与根因诊断能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on OCR-centric document understanding and text-rich visual reasoning benchmarks. Yet existing evaluations largely assume that every task is valid and answerable. In real-world OCR scenarios, this assumption often fails: questions may rely on illegible text, occluded evidence, nonexistent visual targets, contradictory premises, or missing variables. We study this reliability gap as OCR-grounded Task Verification: before answering, a model should determine whether the Image Premise (IP), Textual Premise (TP), and Question (Q) jointly define an executable task.
  We introduce VeriOCRBench, a 1,800-sample human-verified benchmark built from source images drawn from 8 OCR-related datasets and spanning 8 real-world image domains, with controlled, image-grounded diagnostic tasks.
  It contains 1,600 trap-injected invalid tasks across 8 trap types and four verification dimensions---Visual, Contextual, Factual, and Logical---plus 200 trap-free controls for measuring over-refusal. Built with a Visual Atomic Fact (VAF)-anchored pipeline and full human auditing, VeriOCRBench enables decoupled evaluation of task verification, root-cause diagnosis, and over-refusal. Evaluating 15 leading MLLMs reveals persistent blind compliance, diagnosis failures, and prompt-induced over-refusal, exposing a critical reliability gap in current OCR reasoning systems.
  The code is available at: https://github.com/zy001122/Beyond-Blind-Compliance.

</details>


### [8] [CoLT-Drive: Counterfactual Long-Tail Benchmarking and Knowledge-Preserving Adaptation for Driving Affordance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00242)
*Zhengxu Tang,Guofeng Cui,Ziyu Gong,Xiaozhou Zhang,Ruifeng Deng,Chengzhi Qi,Ke Chen,Sachin Patil,Tianjun Xiao,Langechuan Liu,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将长尾自动驾驶失败从“稀有物体识别”推进到“决策级驾驶可供性预测”，并构建 CoLT-Drive 反事实长尾基准；提出 KPA 框架提升小型视觉语言模型在长尾决策场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长尾自动驾驶研究常把问题简化为稀有物体识别，但真正影响安全决策的是模型能否理解异常物体如何改变自车可行的高层纵向与横向动作。因此需要一个面向决策层的评测任务和适配方法。

Method: 作者形式化了决策级驾驶可供性预测任务：输入前视图像、自车运动历史与导航指令，输出结构化的纵向—横向元动作。构建 CoLT-Drive 基准，通过在固定驾驶场景中插入稀有物体生成 3,536 个反事实样本，并评估预测动作对是否可接受。提出 KPA，包括结构化感知到决策提示、基于 SLERP 的专家融合，以及 RegMoE（面向驾驶决策状态的 LoRA 混合专家模块）。

Result: 在域内驾驶划分和 CoLT-Drive 上实验显示，KPA 在 CoLT-Drive 上达到 60.8% 的动作对准确率，优于预训练 Qwen3-VL-2B 基线的 50.3% 和 LoRA SFT 的 32.4%，同时保持有竞争力的域内准确率。

Conclusion: 论文表明，长尾驾驶能力应在决策层面评估，而不仅是识别层面；KPA 能在保留预训练开放世界知识的同时，为不同驾驶决策状态分配轻量适配能力，从而提升小型 VLM 的长尾决策鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-tail autonomous driving failures are often framed as rare-object recognition errors. We argue that this view is incomplete: the decision-critical question is not only whether a model recognizes an unusual object, but whether it infers how that object changes the ego vehicle's feasible high-level actions. We formalize this problem as decision-level driving affordance prediction, where a model maps a front-view image, ego-motion history, and navigation command to a structured longitudinal--lateral meta-action. To evaluate this capability, we introduce CoLT-Drive, a 3,536-sample counterfactual long-tail benchmark that inserts rare objects into otherwise fixed driving scenes and measures whether models predict acceptable action pairs. To improve deployable small VLMs, we propose KPA, a knowledge-preserving adaptation framework that combines structured perception-to-decision prompting, SLERP-based expert merging, and RegMoE, a regime-aware LoRA mixture-of-experts module. KPA preserves the pretrained model's open-world knowledge while allocating lightweight adaptation capacity to different driving decision regimes. Experiments on an in-domain driving split and CoLT-Drive show that KPA achieves 60.8\% pair accuracy on CoLT-Drive, outperforming the pretrained Qwen3-VL-2B baseline (50.3\%) and LoRA SFT (32.4\%) while maintaining competitive in-domain accuracy. Our benchmark and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tangzx2024/CoLT-Drive and https://github.com/tangzhengxu/CoLT-Drive.

</details>


### [9] [CrossFeat: Bridging Imaging Modalities in Feature Descriptor Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00272)
*Paul Schneider,Nazim Haouchine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossFeat 通过在描述子空间学习跨模态映射，使已有单模态关键点描述子无需为每个模态对重新训练即可用于多模态匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关键点描述方法主要面向单模态变化；多模态图像因成像机制不同导致同一结构外观差异显著。为每个模态对训练专用描述子成本高且泛化差，大模型又带来运行开销，因此需要一种轻量、可复用的跨模态适配方案。

Method: 提出 CrossFeat 框架，在描述子空间学习 crossing function，将一种模态的特征映射到与另一模态兼容的表示。方法引入几何—外观解耦机制，仅调整外观相关成分，同时保留原始描述子捕获的几何结构信息。

Result: 在多个领域和数据集上的实验表明，CrossFeat 在多模态匹配任务中取得了更好的性能。

Conclusion: CrossFeat 能有效扩展现有单模态描述子的跨模态适用性，在避免频繁重训和大模型高开销的同时提升多模态关键点匹配表现。

Abstract: Most advances in keypoint descriptions address monomodal settings, where image variations arise from viewpoint, illumination, or contrast changes. Multimodal scenarios involve images produced by fundamentally different sensing processes, such as multispectral imaging, RGB-depth, satellite imagery, or medical imaging, causing the same structures to appear differently. A common solution to cross-modal description is to train descriptors for each modality pair, which requires retraining whenever the modalities change, or to train large models, which incur a significant increase in runtime. Instead, we propose CrossFeat, a framework that enables an existing monomodal descriptor to operate across modalities. Our method learns a crossing function in descriptor space that maps features from one modality to a representation compatible with another. To preserve the structural information captured by the original descriptor, CrossFeat introduces a geometry-appearance disentanglement such that only appearance is altered while the geometric properties are preserved. Experiments across multiple domains and datasets demonstrate improved performance in multimodal matching.

</details>


### [10] [StreamScout: Learning When to Look Deeper for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00291)
*Ce Zhang,Jing Bi,Jinxi He,Jianshu Zhang,Jingyang Lin,Yunzhong Xiao,Minghao Fu,Yaqi Xie,Zhentao Xie,Weicong Chen,Katia Sycara,Ming Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 StreamScout：一种面向无界流式视频问答的自适应推理框架，根据每个问题所需证据动态决定访问记忆的深度，在提升性能的同时显著降低推理成本与 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解方法主要关注在有限记忆中保留什么信息，但对不同问题通常采用固定成本的记忆访问方式；而实际查询对视觉证据的需求差异很大，因此需要按查询自适应决定访问深度。

Method: StreamScout 在视频流过程中仅在上下文中维护轻量文本时间线；查询时按级联方式逐步增加视觉信息：最近帧快速浏览、对历史流的均匀回看、以及与查询相关的显著检索。每一阶段若证据足够则立即回答，否则升级到下一阶段。进一步通过辅助集探测级联能力边界，并用 LoRA 蒸馏得到 StreamScout-S；再通过强化学习优化停止/升级策略，得到 StreamScout-R。

Result: 在三个视觉语言骨干模型和三个流式视频基准上，StreamScout 系列稳定优于既有流式方法，并显著减少推理成本与 token 使用。例如在 OVO-Bench 上，StreamScout-S 使 Qwen3-VL-8B 提升 14.65 分，同时比均匀采样少用 59% token，平均回答时间为 1.04 秒。

Conclusion: 论文表明，流式视频理解中不仅要优化记忆内容，还要优化每个查询的记忆访问深度；自适应级联访问与学习化停止策略能在准确率、延迟和计算成本之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires answering questions that arrive at arbitrary moments over an unbounded video stream. Existing systems primarily focus on what to retain in a bounded memory, yet access that memory using the same fixed-cost procedure for every query, despite substantial variation in the evidence required. We argue that deciding how deeply to access memory for each query is as important as deciding what the memory should store. To this end, we introduce StreamScout, an adaptive inference framework that maintains only a lightweight textual timeline in context as the stream unfolds. At query time, StreamScout progressively augments the timeline with up to three increasingly informative visual views: a glance at recent frames, a uniform look-back over the past stream, and query-salient retrieval. At each stage, the model answers immediately if the available evidence is sufficient; otherwise, it escalates to the next view. To improve this stop-or-escalate policy, we probe the cascade on an auxiliary set and distill the model's empirical competence boundary into supervision for a lightweight LoRA adaptation, yielding StreamScout-S. We further refine the policy through reinforcement learning, allowing the model to explore stopping behaviors beyond imitation of the distilled decisions, yielding StreamScout-R. Across three backbones and three streaming benchmarks, StreamScout and its variants consistently outperform prior streaming methods while substantially reducing inference cost and token consumption; on OVO-Bench, for instance, StreamScout-S improves Qwen3-VL-8B by 14.65 points while using 59% fewer tokens than uniform sampling and answering in 1.04 s on average.

</details>


### [11] [TRUST: Threshold-Recalibrated Uncertainty-Safe Training for Certified Dismissal in Breast Cancer Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00300)
*Parham Hajishafiezahramini,Matthew Hamilton,Edward Kendall,Gregory Doyle,Oscar Meruvia Pastor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种闭环、阈值感知训练策略，用于在保持高癌症召回率的前提下自动剔除明确阴性的乳腺筛查影像，从而降低放射科医生阅片负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌筛查中大量影像为明确阴性，若能安全地减少这些病例的人工复核，可显著降低工作量；关键挑战是在不损害癌症检出率的情况下实现高置信度选择性剔除。

Method: 方法在训练过程中动态重新计算剔除阈值，并将该阈值反馈到损失设计中，对接近剔除区域的癌症阳性图像施加惩罚。研究在 NLBS 和 RSNA 数据集上设置五种受控训练配置，并采用病例级评估；被剔除病例中的癌症患病率通过单侧 99% Clopper–Pearson 上界进行约束评估。

Result: 所提模型在 98% 和 95% 召回目标下均取得最高病例级剔除率。NLBS 上剔除率分别为 19.74% 和 21.70%，而交叉熵基线未达到对应召回目标。RSNA 上，剔除率分别从 7.04% 提升至 14.31%，以及从 13.49% 提升至 19.69%。外部 RSNA→NLBS 测试中，模型在 98% 和 95% 召回目标下分别达到 12.95% 和 19.87% 的剔除率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，闭环阈值感知训练能够有效提升高召回场景下的选择性阴性病例剔除能力，并具有一定外部泛化潜力。

Abstract: Reducing the review of clearly cancer-negative screening mammograms could lower radiologist workload without compromising cancer detection. We propose a closed-loop threshold-aware training strategy in which the dismissal threshold is recalculated during training and used to penalize cancer-positive images that approach the dismissal region. We evaluated the method on NLBS and RSNA using five controlled training configurations, with case-level assessment based on a one-sided 99\% Clopper--Pearson upper bound for cancer prevalence among dismissed cases. The proposed model achieved the highest case-level dismissal rates at both 98\% and 95\% recall targets. On NLBS, dismissal reached 19.74\% and 21.70\%, while the cross-entropy baseline did not meet either recall target. On RSNA, dismissal improved from 7.04\% to 14.31\% and from 13.49\% to 19.69\%. In external RSNA$\to$NLBS evaluation, the proposed model achieved dismissal rates of 12.95\% and 19.87\% at the 98\% and 95\% recall targets, respectively. These results support closed-loop threshold-aware training for high-recall selective dismissal.

</details>


### [12] [SlideMix: Enhancing Whole Slide Image Analysis via Multimodal Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00396)
*Chad Wong,Sicheng Chen,Tianyi Zhang,Enhui Chai,Yueming Jin,Zeyu Liu,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlideMix 是一种面向 MIL 式病理全切片图像分析的即插即用增强框架，利用视觉语言模型选择诊断相关区域、进行上下文保持的特征混合，并通过软标签与课程学习提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI 具有超高分辨率、组织异质性、弱监督标签、诊断区域稀疏和多尺度证据等特点，导致自动分析困难；现有数据增强方法常在组织区域上盲目扰动，难以保持诊断相关性、切片上下文和跨尺度结构。

Method: 提出 SlideMix：首先使用检索增强的 VLM 视觉-语言自适应区域选择器定位诊断相关区域以降低弱标签噪声；随后在有意义组织区域内执行原位 tile shuffle，在保留切片级上下文的同时混合特征嵌入；再用 VLM 软标签模块监督混合样本；最后通过多因素、损失驱动的在线课程学习反馈机制自适应控制 shuffle 粒度、特征相似度和 shuffle 比例。

Result: 在 11 个 WSI 数据集、20,523 张切片、8 个诊断任务和 10 种 WSI backbone 上，SlideMix 在多数设置中提升了准确率和泛化能力，并优于或媲美已有增强基线。

Conclusion: SlideMix 为 MIL-based WSI 分析提供了一种模型无关、可扩展、易集成的增强方案，能够更好地利用诊断相关区域和跨尺度信息，从而提升数字病理模型的鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Histopathological whole slide images (WSIs) are central to cancer diagnosis, but their gigapixel scale, tissue heterogeneity, weak slide-level supervision, sparse diagnostic regions, and multi-scale evidence make robust automated analysis challenging. Multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely used to aggregate tile-level features into slide-level predictions, yet existing augmentation strategies often perturb tissue regions without preserving diagnostic relevance, slide context, or cross-scale structure. We propose SlideMix, a model-agnostic multimodal augmentation framework for MIL-based WSI analysis. SlideMix uses a retrieval-augmented vision-language model (VLM)-based Visual-Language Adaptive Region selector to identify diagnostically relevant regions and reduce weak-label noise. It then performs In-place Tile Shuffling within meaningful tissue regions to mix feature embeddings while preserving slide-level context. A VLM-based soft-labeling module supervises mixed samples, while a multi-factor, loss-driven online Curriculum-Learning Feedback scheme adaptively controls shuffle granularity, feature similarity, and shuffle ratio to promote cross-scale representation learning. Across 11 WSI datasets comprising 20,523 slides, 8 diagnostic tasks, and 10 WSI backbones, SlideMix improves accuracy and generalization in most settings and compares favorably with established augmentation baselines, providing a simple plug-and-play approach for more robust and scalable digital pathology models. Source code: https://github.com/Xia-Research-Lab/SlideMix

</details>


### [13] [Unmasking Face Embeddings: Reading, Rendering and Naming with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00411)
*Fizza Rubab,Yiying Tong,Arun Ross*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出用预计算的简单线性变换，将人脸识别模型的身份嵌入与现成基础模型的嵌入空间对齐，从而让原本不透明的人脸模板具备文本描述、检索、图像重建和姓名推断等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代人脸识别模型能生成高效的身份判别嵌入，但语义可解释性较弱；基础模型则具备丰富的视觉/语言接口。论文关注的问题是：如果将专用人脸识别嵌入与基础模型互操作，会释放哪些新能力，并带来哪些安全与隐私影响。

Method: 基于模型间嵌入兼容性的思想，使用成对嵌入估计简单的预计算线性变换；不训练或修改原有人脸识别模型和基础模型，仅通过线性映射把人脸身份嵌入对齐到基础模型空间。对齐后，可利用基础模型进行自然语言读取、文本查询、扩散解码重建人脸图像，以及姓名转换/识别。

Result: 一个线性变换即可把紧凑的人脸身份嵌入转化为可被基础模型利用的丰富表征，使其支持自由文本检索、外观重建、语义解释和无注册图库情况下的身份名称推断等功能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，人脸识别嵌入并非只是抽象身份模板，而是包含丰富语义和视觉信息；与基础模型对齐后显著提升可解释性和应用能力，同时暴露出模板安全、隐私泄露和生物特征保护方面的重要风险。

Abstract: Modern face recognition (FR) owes much of its success to deep neural networks that learn to extract compact identity embeddings from face images. These models are typically trained for identity discrimination, producing embeddings that are highly effective for biometric matching but largely opaque to semantic interpretation. In contrast, foundation models, pretrained on broad visual or vision--language tasks, provide rich interfaces for describing, retrieving, generating, and organizing visual content. This contrast raises a natural question: what capabilities become available when face embeddings from domain-specific FR models are made interoperable with foundation models? Building on recent work on embedding compatibility across models, we use simple pre-computed linear transformations, estimated from paired embeddings alone, to connect existing FR models with off-the-shelf foundation models. Once aligned with a foundation model, a face embedding can be 'unmasked' in multiple ways, without training or modifying either model: it can be read in natural language, enabling free-form text queries over a gallery of FR embeddings; rendered into a face image that recovers a person's appearance, using an unmodified diffusion decoder; and converted to a name, enabling identification even in the absence of an enrolled face gallery. In effect, one linear transformation turns an identity embedding into a rich embedding for web-scale foundation models. This interoperability exposes face embeddings as semantically and visually rich biometric representations, with direct implications for interpretability, retrieval, reconstruction, and template security.

</details>


### [14] [Instance-Guided Report Anchoring for Text-Free 3D Abnormality Segmentation in Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00447)
*Zhenyu Bu,Haoyan Ding,Chushu Shen,Xinyuan Zheng,Peiyu Duan,Xueqi Guo,Sepehr Farhand,Yoshihisa Shinagawa,Gerardo Hermosillo,Chaowei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 IGRA：一种训练阶段利用放射报告中实例级描述来增强 3D 胸部 CT 异常分割、推理阶段无需文本输入的模型无关模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 胸部 CT 异常分割需要密集体素级标注，专家标注成本高；放射报告天然包含异常实例的描述，可作为额外监督信号。但现有视觉-语言定位方法推理时通常仍依赖文本查询，限制了实用性和一次前向可预测的目标数量。

Method: IGRA 保留每个已标注异常实例与对应报告发现之间的配对关系：训练时对实例特征进行池化，并将其锚定到相应文本发现嵌入；推理时移除所有文本相关组件。作者还将 ReXGroundingCT 上的自由文本 grounding 任务重构为多标签体积分割任务，通过合并同类别实例，使模型能够在一次仅图像输入的前向传播中预测全部异常类别。

Result: IGRA 相比最强 image-only 基线 Dice 提升 22.5%，从 25.25 提升到 30.93；在 single-finding 子集上与 VoxTell 表现接近，Dice 为 30.29 vs. 30.43。该模块应用于四种标准 3D 分割骨干网络时，均提升 Dice 和 hit rate。零样本评估在 LIDC-IDRI、PleThora 及私有内部数据集上也较 image-only 基线获得一致增益。

Conclusion: IGRA 证明了报告中的实例级语义信息可在训练阶段有效增强 3D 医学图像分割，同时保持推理阶段的图像-only、高效率和多类别一次性预测能力，具有较好的模型泛化性和跨数据集迁移潜力。

Abstract: Accurate 3D abnormality segmentation in chest CT requires dense spatial supervision, but obtaining expert voxel-level labels is costly. Radiology reports, however, are routinely generated during clinical interpretation and contain instance-specific descriptions that can provide additional guidance without new dense annotation. Existing vision-language grounding methods typically require report-derived findings at inference, making localization dependent on paired text and limiting each forward pass to a queried finding. We propose Instance-Guided Report Anchoring (IGRA), a model-agnostic module that preserves the correspondence between each annotated abnormality instance and the report finding that describes it. IGRA pools each instance representation and anchors it to the corresponding finding embedding during training; all text-related components are discarded at inference. We further reformulate free-text grounding on ReXGroundingCT as multi-label volumetric segmentation by merging same-category instances, allowing all abnormality categories to be predicted in one image-only forward pass. IGRA improves Dice by 22.5% over the strongest image-only baseline (30.93 vs. 25.25) and is comparable to VoxTell on the single-finding subset (30.29 vs. 30.43). Applied unchanged to four standard 3D segmentation backbones, IGRA improves Dice and hit rate across all architectures. Zero-shot evaluation on LIDC-IDRI, PleThora, and a private in-house dataset further shows consistent gains over image-only baselines.

</details>


### [15] [Less Is More: Balancing Positive and Negative Space in Visual Concept Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00476)
*Shishi Xiao,Adam J. Coscia,David H. Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化图形概念融合管线，显式利用正负空间来生成更具表现力、创造性且易识别的混合视觉设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动概念融合方法虽能辅助设计，但通常忽视空间构图，尤其是正空间与负空间在平衡、强调和审美表达中的作用。论文旨在弥补这一缺口，使计算系统能够像设计师一样通过空间关系融合多个视觉概念。

Method: 方法首先结合视觉-语言模型的语义推理与来自真实样例的几何约束，识别适合概念整合的区域；随后采用混合像素-矢量管线生成融合构图：先用扩散模型补全快速产生粗略初始化，再通过点级矢量优化提升结构一致性和语义平衡。整个流程由多模态智能体作为规划器与评估器进行调度，实现迭代改进和可解释控制。

Result: 通过与基线方法对比及用户研究，实验表明该方法在表达力、创造性和概念可识别性方面表现更优，并能有效利用正负空间完成视觉概念融合。

Conclusion: 论文证明，将正负空间显式纳入自动概念融合流程能够显著提升图形设计质量；该框架具有较好的泛化能力，可应用于可控图像生成、信息图生成等多种设计场景。

Abstract: Graphic designers often blend visual concepts to communicate multiple ideas within a single image, leveraging positive and negative space to create balance, emphasis, and aesthetic appeal. While computational methods have begun to support automatic concept blending, they largely overlook the role of spatial composition in the design. To address this gap, we present an automatic pipeline that explicitly applies positive and negative space throughout the blending process. Our approach first identifies plausible regions for concept integration by combining semantic reasoning from vision-language models with geometric constraints derived from real-world examples. Conditioned on these regions, the system generates blended compositions using a hybrid pixel-vector pipeline: diffusion-based inpainting produces a fast, coarse initialization, which is then refined through vector-based optimization at the point level to ensure structural coherence and balanced semantic expression. A multimodal agent orchestrates this process as a planner and evaluator, enabling iterative improvement and interpretable control. Through an evaluation using both baseline comparisons and a user study, we demonstrate greater expressiveness, creativity, and concept recognizability by effectively leveraging positive and negative space. We further demonstrate the generalizability of our approach across diverse applications, including controllable image and infographic generation.

</details>


### [16] [ViTAL-X: Video-Text Alignment with Cross-Modal Temporal Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00505)
*Sethuraman T,Savya Khosla,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Aditi Tiwari,Seoung Wug Oh,Kushal Kafle,Joon-Young Lee,Derek Hoiem,Simon Jenni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有由图文模型迁移而来的视频-文本模型普遍存在“时间盲视”，并提出XTE-Bench用于诊断该问题；进一步提出自监督框架XTE，通过跨模态时间编辑生成高质量时间负样本，训练轻量模型ViTAL-X，在多个时间推理基准上以更少参数和数据取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-文本模型常继承CLIP等图文架构，容易依赖静态空间线索，导致难以理解顺序、方向、运动动态等基础时间信息；同时标准数据集可能掩盖这一缺陷。因此需要更系统的诊断基准和更有效的时间监督方法，而不是单纯依赖模型规模扩张。

Method: 作者提出XTE-Bench作为诊断性评测基准，用于暴露视频语言模型的时间推理缺陷。为改进模型，提出Cross-Modal Temporal Edits（XTE）自监督框架，通过同步的视频与文本时间变换构造困难时间负样本，无需人工标注。基于该框架实现ViTAL-X，在冻结图文骨干的基础上加入轻量时间感知模块，以保留空间表征能力并增强时间理解。

Result: 实验显示，大规模视频语言模型在XTE-Bench上仍难以完成基本时间推理，说明仅靠参数扩展不足以解决时间盲视。ViTAL-X在六个时间基准上达到最先进性能；仅使用0.4B参数和100万训练视频片段，就超过7B参数模型，并优于使用多达600倍训练数据的基线。

Conclusion: 论文表明，视频-文本模型的关键瓶颈之一是缺乏精确时间对齐与监督。相比单纯扩大参数和数据规模，面向时间语义的高质量自监督信号更高效；XTE与ViTAL-X为增强视频语言模型的时间理解提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Video-text models adapted from image-text architectures (e.g., CLIP) frequently exhibit temporal blindness, the inability to perceive fundamental cues like order, direction, and motion dynamics. Standard datasets mask this limitation by enabling models to exploit static spatial shortcuts. To systematically evaluate this, we introduce XTE-Bench, a diagnostic probe revealing that even large-scale video-language models struggle with basic temporal reasoning, indicating that parameter scaling alone is insufficient to resolve this flaw. To address this, we propose Cross-Modal Temporal Edits (XTE), a self-supervised framework that injects precise temporal supervision. By performing synchronized video-text transformations, XTE generates hard temporal negatives without manual annotation. We instantiate this with ViTAL-X, a lightweight model that equips frozen image-text backbones with temporal awareness while preserving their foundational spatial knowledge. Across six temporal benchmarks, ViTAL-X achieves state-of-the-art performance. Utilizing only 0.4B parameters and 1M training clips, ViTAL-X outperforms 7B-parameter models and surpasses baselines trained on 600x more data. These results demonstrate that targeted, high-quality temporal alignment provides a highly efficient alternative to pure scaling.

</details>


### [17] [GenScale: A Benchmark for Relative Object Scale in Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00525)
*Lingxiao Li,Max Whitton,Ledell Wu,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenScale 基准与评估协议，用于衡量图像生成/编辑系统中的相对物体尺度合理性，并提出后处理方法 Rescale 改善尺度错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现代图像生成与编辑系统能生成逼真且符合提示的图像，但仍常将熟悉物体渲染为不合理的相对尺寸；现有评估较少将真实世界相对尺度作为独立能力进行系统测量。

Method: 作者构建 GenScale，包含 900 个图像级条目与 1,643 个锚点-目标成对尺度关系，覆盖常见物体生成、带公制尺寸的人-产品生成，以及失败生成结果的尺度修正；同时设计人类校准的序数评判器进行可扩展的成对尺度评估，并提出模型无关的局部尺度修正后处理代理 Rescale。

Result: 实验表明，当前先进图像生成器和编辑器仍无法稳定遵守相对尺度约束；Rescale 能在生成图像与编辑图像中持续提升尺度合理性。

Conclusion: GenScale 将相对物体尺度确立为图像生成系统中一个独立、可测量且可干预的能力维度；Rescale 证明了无需修改源生成模型也能有效改善局部尺度错误。

Abstract: Modern image generation and editing systems can produce photorealistic, prompt-aligned images, but still often render familiar objects at implausible relative sizes. To measure this failure mode, we introduce GenScale, a benchmark and evaluation protocol for real-world relative object scale in image generation and editing. GenScale contains 900 image-level entries and 1,643 pairwise anchor-target scale relations across common-object generation, human-product generation with metric dimensions, and scale correction from failed generations. We further design a human-calibrated ordinal judge for scalable pairwise scale evaluation. Last but not the least, we introduce Rescale, a model-agnostic post-processing agent for localized scale correction without modifying the source generator. Experiments reveal that state-of-the-art image generators and editors cannot reliably observe relative scale yet, while Rescale consistently improves scale plausibility across generated and edited images. Together, GenScale establishes relative object scale as a distinct, measurable, and actionable capability for image generation systems.

</details>


### [18] [A Glance Is All You Need: Single-Pass Fine-Grained Image Captioning with SimLoss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00591)
*Suryaansh Jain,Rahasya Barkur,Vishal G,Ryan Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Jack Wang,Koustava Goswami,Nedim Lipka,Puneet Mathur,Samyadeep Basu,Seunghyun Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimLoss，一种无需参考文本的嵌入空间监督目标，用于单阶段细粒度图像描述，在保持低推理延迟的同时提升描述细节质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型生成的图像描述通常流畅但过于高层，容易遗漏属性、数量、纹理、材质和空间关系等细节；多阶段方法虽能补充细节，但推理延迟显著增加。

Method: SimLoss 通过 InfoNCE 对比损失，将视觉语言模型的投影隐藏状态与冻结图像嵌入对齐，在文本解码前提供密集视觉监督。方法不依赖人工细粒度标注或多阶段伪描述。论文实现了两种变体：SimLoss FFT 通过本地嵌入模型进行可微反向传播；SimLoss GRPO 将嵌入模型作为黑盒奖励进行优化。

Result: 与单阶段、多阶段验证、奖励优化和感知增强等基线相比，SimLoss FFT 获得最高精确率，并且 F1 分数接近多阶段方法，同时保持单阶段推理，速度约为多阶段流水线的 20 倍。SimLoss GRPO 则取得最强召回率。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间监督能够在单阶段图像描述器的低延迟条件下，恢复接近多阶段验证方法的细粒度描述质量，表明其是高效细粒度图像描述训练的有效方向。

Abstract: An image may be worth a thousand words, but most captioning models describe it in only a few. Modern vision-language models produce fluent high-level captions, yet routinely miss the attributes, counts, textures, materials, and spatial relations that make an image visually specific. Recent multi-stage systems recover some of these details through generation, decomposition, verification, and rewriting, but they do so at the expense of substantially higher inference latency.
  We propose SimLoss, a reference-free embedding-space objective for single-pass fine-grained image captioning. SimLoss trains a vision-language model to align its projected hidden-state representation with a frozen image embedding through an InfoNCE contrastive loss, supplying a dense visual supervision signal before any text is decoded, and requiring neither human-written fine-grained captions nor pseudo-captions from a multi-stage pipeline. We instantiate it as SimLoss FFT, which backpropagates through a locally available embedding model, and SimLoss GRPO, which treats that model as a black-box reward.
  Compared with single-pass, multi-stage verification, reward-optimized, and perception-aware baselines, the fully differentiable fine-tuning variant, SimLoss FFT, achieves the highest precision while nearly matching the F1 score of the multi-stage method, all while retaining single-pass inference and running roughly 20 times faster than the multi-stage pipeline.
  The reward-based variant SimLoss GRPO attains the strongest recall. Together, these results show that embedding-space supervision can recover the quality of multi-stage verification at the latency of a single-pass captioner.

</details>


### [19] [BrainDiff: Longitudinal Report Generation for Multimodal Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00593)
*Krish Patel,Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrainDiff 是首个面向脑部 MRI 纵向报告生成的视觉-语言系统，能利用同一患者的前后影像与既往报告生成更可靠的随访报告，并在内部与外部队列上优于通用模型和单次检查神经影像模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床神经放射科医生通常会结合患者既往脑 MRI 进行阅片，而现有自动脑 MRI 报告生成方法几乎都针对单次检查。脑 MRI 的间隔变化往往细微且空间分布广，胸片或胸部 CT 中的时序分析方法不能直接解决该场景，因此需要专门的纵向脑 MRI 报告生成系统。

Method: 作者提出 BrainDiff，一个纵向脑 MRI 视觉-语言系统。方法上利用患者成对脑 MRI 及既往报告，并引入两类增强影像依据的机制：带有既往报告 dropout 的反事实目标，以及分阶段课程学习。同时，作者进行因素实验以区分既往报告可用性和影像身份的影响，并提出低成本的变化可解码性测试来评估候选视觉骨干网络对间隔变化的捕捉能力。

Result: BrainDiff 在同一患者配对数据上超过前沿通用模型和单次检查神经影像模型；在外部跨医院队列上保留了内部 RadGraph-XL 实体+关系 F1（rg_er）的 91%。反事实目标结合既往报告 dropout 使测得的影像依赖提升约 47%，与分阶段课程学习结合后，相比基线使影像依赖提升 2.5 倍。因素实验显示视觉信息带来 +0.0387 rg_er 的贡献，且在缺少既往报告时贡献更大。变化可解码性测试表明，间隔变化比单次病变更难解码，AUROC 为 0.60 对 0.77。

Conclusion: 该工作证明纵向建模对脑 MRI 报告生成具有重要价值，并提出了提升模型真实利用影像信息的方法。BrainDiff 不仅提高了纵向脑 MRI 报告生成性能，还提供了评估影像贡献和变化可解码性的分析框架，提示未来模型需要更强地捕捉细微、分布式的时序变化。

Abstract: Neuroradiologists rarely read a brain MRI in isolation, yet automated brain-MRI report generation has been built almost entirely for single studies. Temporal analysis has been explored on chest radiography and chest CT, but to our knowledge, longitudinal reporting for brain MRI, where interval change is often subtle and spatially distributed, remains unaddressed. We present BrainDiff, the first longitudinal vision-language system for brain MRI. BrainDiff outperforms both frontier general-purpose and single-study neuroimaging models on the same patient pairs. Moreover, BrainDiff retains 91% of internal RadGraph-XL entity+relation F1 (rg_er) on an external, cross-hospital cohort. Beyond the system, we contribute three analyses. First, we identify two independent grounding levers: a counterfactual objective with prior-report dropout, which increases measured image reliance by ~47%, and a staged curriculum. Together, these interventions raise image reliance 2.5-fold from the baseline. Second, we provide a factorial over prior-report availability and image identity, isolating a visual contribution of +0.0387 rg_er, which grows when the prior report is withheld. Third, a cheap change-decodability test for candidate backbones shows that interval change is decodable far more weakly than single-study pathology (0.60 vs. 0.77 AUROC). Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jhuldr/BrainDiff.

</details>


### [20] [Restrict, Don't Retrain: Inference-Time VLM Guidance for Zero-Shot Aerial Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00628)
*Teresa DiMeola,Charles Walter,Hong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向航拍/卫星图像语义分割的VLM辅助框架，通过为基础分割模型补充类别选择与小目标定位信息，提升像素级分割效果并提供可审计证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用预训练基础模型直接用于遥感图像时，可能漏检场景中的关键类别，尤其是小目标；而实际应用需要精确的像素级定位与可靠解释。

Method: 方法融合三部分：使用冻结的基础模型进行全像素标注；调用视觉语言模型进行一次查询以确定场景中重要类别；再进行一次查询以定位基础模型容易遗漏的小目标，并将这些信息用于改进分割结果。

Result: 在四个航拍数据集上的评估显示，只要基础模型具备一定能力，该方法在各个增强阶段均带来稳定性能提升。

Conclusion: VLM可作为轻量、可审计的指导模块，在单张消费级GPU条件下增强遥感图像分割，尤其有助于补足类别识别与小目标定位方面的不足。

Abstract: Global welfare often depends on the correct interpretation of aerial and satellite imagery. Acting on such imagery (mapping flooded ground, crop extent, or damaged infrastructure) demands pixel-level segmentation to ensure perfect class localization. Pretrained general foundation models, when applied directly, often miss important features and cannot always find all the classes belonging to a given scene, overlooking smaller objects that matter most. We use a single consumer-grade GPU running a vision-language model (VLM) to supply this missing guidance, improving segmentation while producing structured, auditable evidence that drives the result and can be inspected on its own. We fuse three approaches: the frozen foundation model that labels every pixel, and two queries to a VLM, one to choose the classes that matter, and one to locate the small objects the base model misses. Evaluating across four aerial datasets, we see consistent gains at each stage where the base model is competent.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Landmark Extraction: A Framework for Robust Geometric Feature Construction in Structured Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00634)
*Saravana Mauree,Sakshi Arya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将结构化图像识别的重点从“选择哪种分类器”转向“分类器应基于何种表征学习”，提出并评估基于关键点的特征构造框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手势识别、表情分类、医学图像分析等结构化视觉任务中，判别信息往往来自语义部件之间的空间关系，而非单个像素。原始像素空间维度高、易受无关变化影响，且难以呈现可解释的几何结构；仅提取关键点也不足以决定保留哪些有效信息，因此需要系统研究关键点之后的特征映射。

Method: 以静态手势识别为案例，围绕关键点派生表征构建分析框架，比较坐标、距离、角度以及混合表征，并通过扰动实验和消融实验评估不同特征在视觉变化条件下的鲁棒性与分类效果。

Result: 实验表明，在视觉变化较大的数据中，原始坐标特征与具备几何不变性的特征之间存在明显性能差距；混合表征通过结合互补的几何信息取得最佳整体表现。

Conclusion: 特征构造是结构化视觉建模中的基础决策。相比单纯比较分类器，研究分类器应从何种表征中学习同样关键；基于关键点的几何特征映射可作为一种有信息约束的特征型降维步骤。

Abstract: Much of the literature on structured image recognition has disproportionately focused on the comparison of classification algorithms. Rather than investigating which classifier performs best, this paper instead asks: what should a classifier know before it ever makes a prediction? In structured vision problems such as gesture recognition, facial expression categorization, and medical image analysis, discriminative information lies less in individual pixels and more in spatial relationships between semantic parts. Raw pixel spaces are high-dimensional, sensitive to nuisance variation, and often obfuscate the geometric structures that make visual tasks interpretable. Landmark extraction provides one form of dimension reduction, but it does not by itself determine the information preserved. This paper studies the post-landmark feature map as the central object of analysis and proposes a systematic framework for constructing and interpreting landmark-derived representations as an, informed, feature-based "dimension reduction" step. Using static hand gesture recognition as a case study, we evaluate coordinate, distance, angle, and hybrid representations through perturbation and ablation experiments. The results show that visually variable data exposes substantial gaps between raw coordinate features and their geometrically invariant counterparts, while hybrid representations achieve the strongest overall performance by combining complementary geometric components. These findings frame feature construction as a fundamental modeling decision and ultimately suggests that the question of what representation should a classifier learn from is one worth asking. The code used for feature construction and evaluation is available at https://github.com/ShivMaureeCWRU/Feature_based_dimension_reduction

</details>


### [22] [You Cannot Photograph the Same Street Twice: Reliability Limits in Vision-Language Measurement of Urban Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00649)
*Kaizhen Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估视觉-语言模型基于重复街景图像测量城市变化的纵向可靠性，发现个体点位可靠性较差，但在数百个配对样本聚合后可恢复一致的重建/变化信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型正被用于从多时相街景图像中量化城市环境变化，但同一街道在未发生显著重建时，模型感知评分会因重拍、图像处理、提示顺序、相机条件等因素产生多大波动尚不清楚。

Method: 研究使用美国五个城市中435个Google Street View站点的4,648个连续时期图像对，比较无显著重建街道的感知评分变化；分解重复调用、图像重编码、提示顺序等来源的方差；检验六类图像统计特征的解释力；并通过受控实验与众包图像实验评估采集条件、相机几何和虚拟相机归一化的影响。

Result: 同一街道重拍会导致感知评分平均变化0.80分，相当于同城不同街道差异的66.5%。重复模型调用几乎不贡献变异，图像重编码和提示顺序变化各约占同城街道间差异的五分之一；图像统计特征几乎无法解释剩余跨时期波动。仍存在约0.1分的小系统漂移，并随拍摄间隔增加而增大。众包图像中，仅相机几何差异就使模型在45%的相同场景对中报告物理变化；统一虚拟相机后降至7.5%。聚合层面上，变化街道被判断为更富裕、维护更好、更封闭且绿化更少。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型用于城市变化测量时，在单个地点或单个样本点层面纵向可靠性不足，容易受重拍和采集条件影响；但在数百个配对观测的聚合尺度上，仍能提供较一致、可解释的城市重建信号。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used to measure urban change from repeated street-level imagery, but their longitudinal reliability is not well understood. We test how much a perception score can change when the street itself does not undergo substantial redevelopment. Using 4,648 consecutive-epoch image pairs from 435 Google Street View standpoints across five US cities, we find that re-photographing the same street changes a perception score by 0.80 points on average, equivalent to 66.5% of the difference between two different streets in the same city. Repeated model calls contribute almost no variation, while image re-encoding and prompt-order changes each account for about one fifth of the between-street difference. Six image statistics describing scattering, contrast, colour, exposure, sharpness and specularity explain almost none of the remaining epoch-to-epoch variation. A small systematic drift of about 0.1 points remains and increases with the interval between captures, consistent with minor physical changes not recorded by redevelopment labels. Controlled experiments further show that acquisition conditions can shift scores when camera and image properties are allowed to vary, and that the direction of these shifts depends on the model. In crowdsourced imagery, camera geometry alone causes a model to report physical change in 45% of identical-scene pairs; normalising both images to a common virtual camera reduces this rate to 7.5%. Despite poor reliability at the individual-location level, aggregation recovers a coherent redevelopment signal: changed streets are judged wealthier, better maintained, more enclosed and less green. These results show that vision-language measurement of urban change is reliable at the scale of hundreds of paired observations, but not at the scale of individual sample points.

</details>


### [23] [Physically Plausible Video Generation via Visual-Semantic Chain-of-Events Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00656)
*Zixuan Wang,Yixin Hu,Wen Li,Feng Chen,Yan Liu,Duo Peng,Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种事件中心的物理合理视频生成框架，将物理演化建模为因果相连、受物理约束的事件链，以提升生成视频的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PPVG 方法依赖自然语言提示或 CoT 增强提示，但这些提示通常整体性描述物理现象，缺乏对中间状态、事件边界和状态转移动力学的细粒度刻画，导致生成视频容易出现物理不合理的动态。

Method: 方法包含三部分：1）物理驱动事件链推理，将物理过程分解为由演化场景图表示的因果事件链，并将公式推导的物理量绑定到对象与交互上；2）转移感知路由关键帧条件，根据事件类型调用专门的关键帧合成算子，并在去噪过程中用连续关键帧作为残差引导以增强平滑过渡；3）物理注入的对比语义引导，构造物理合理正提示与反事实负提示，通过 classifier-free guidance 引导模型远离违背物理规律的生成结果。

Result: 在 PhyGenBench、VideoPhy、PhyWorldBench 和 Physics-IQ 等基准上，实验显示该框架在多领域场景中生成的视频具有更强的物理合理性。

Conclusion: 将物理过程显式表示为事件链，并结合关键帧转移控制与物理对比语义引导，可以有效缓解自然语言条件不足的问题，提升视频生成的物理一致性与动态可信度。

Abstract: Physically Plausible Video Generation (PPVG) seeks to synthesize videos consistent with physical principles, yet remains challenging due to underspecified natural language conditioning. Advanced chain-of-thought (CoT) frameworks augment prompts with physical knowledge. However, such prompts describe physical phenomena holistically, overlooking intermediate states and transition dynamics. In this paper, we reformulate PPVG as event-centric generation by representing physical evolution as a chain of causally connected and physically constrained events. Our framework comprises three key modules: (1) Physics-driven Event Chain Reasoning. This module decomposes physical phenomena into causally connected events represented by evolving scene graphs. Formula-derived physical quantities are bound to relevant objects and interactions, characterizing the direction and magnitude of each event transition. (2) Transition-aware Routed Keyframe Conditioning. This module routes each event to a specialized keyframe synthesis operator for appearance variation or object transformation. Consecutive keyframes are injected as residual guidance during denoising, enabling smooth visual transitions between event-boundary states. (3) Physics-injected Contrastive Semantic Guidance. This module constructs physics-informed positive and counterfactual negative prompts for classifier-free guidance, steering generation toward plausible dynamics and away from physics-violating counterparts. Experiments on PhyGenBench, VideoPhy, PhyWorldBench, and Physics-IQ demonstrate that our framework generates videos with superior physical plausibility across diverse domains.

</details>


### [24] [Teaching Vision-Language Models to Use the Scale They Are Given: Label-Free Equivariance Training for Metric Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00658)
*Kaizhen Tan,Yang Feng,Heqing Du,Siru Tao,Xin Xu,Hanzhe Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现现有视觉语言模型在回答视频中的度量问题时，不能充分利用给定的真实世界尺度信息；提出无需人工标注的 EquiSD，通过尺度等变约束进行自监督微调，显著提升模型的度量 grounding 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频度量问答要求模型根据提示中的现实参照物或尺度信息，将视觉测量转换为物理单位。但作者观察到，当前模型对尺度变化反应不足：即使所有世界空间量按同一倍数缩放，正确答案也应同比例变化，模型预测却只部分变化，并偏向常见物体尺度。因此需要一种方法改进模型对物理尺度的理解与利用。

Method: 作者利用精确的尺度等变关系作为监督信号：当输入中的世界空间量统一缩放时，正确的度量答案也必须按相同比例缩放。提出 EquiSD，将模型自身预测投影到满足尺度等变性的答案族上，并用生成的目标对模型微调。该方法不需要真实度量标注，每个训练视频只需一次模型查询。

Result: 在保留的模拟视频上，EquiSD 将 3B 模型的中位响应斜率从 0.66 提升到 0.94，并使跨尺度平均相对准确率提升 9.2 个百分点。该方法学到的尺度关系可泛化到未见过的世界尺度，并能无适配迁移到真实 QuantiPhy 视频，准确率提升 6.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，视觉语言模型的主要短板并非物理机制知识，而是度量 grounding 能力不足。利用精确物理对称性（尺度等变性）可以构造无需标签的监督信号，从而有效提升模型在视频度量问题中的尺度理解与物理单位推理能力。

Abstract: Metric questions about video require vision-language models to use supplied real-world references to convert visual measurements into physical units. Yet we find that current models use this scale information only partially. When every world-space quantity in a prompt is rescaled by a common factor, the video remains equally valid and the correct answer changes by exactly that factor, but model predictions move only part of the way and accuracy remains concentrated near the familiar scale of the depicted objects. Across eight vision-language models, this under-response persists over four orders of magnitude. The same models recover the correct closed-form scaling laws when the identical physics is asked in a scale-free form, indicating that the main deficit lies in metric grounding rather than physical mechanism knowledge. We use this exact scaling relation as supervision without requiring metric annotations. Under a common rescaling of the supplied world-space quantities, the correct metric answer must change by the same factor. EquiSD exploits this constraint by projecting a model's own prediction onto the scale-equivariant family and fine-tuning the model on the resulting targets. It requires no ground-truth answers and only one model query per training video. On held-out simulated videos, EquiSD increases a 3B model's median response slope from 0.66 to 0.94 and improves mean relative accuracy by 9.2 points across scales. The learned relation generalizes to unseen world scales and transfers without adaptation to real QuantiPhy videos, where accuracy increases by 6.4 points. These results show that an exact physical symmetry can provide label-free supervision for improving metric grounding in vision-language models.

</details>


### [25] [Do Satellites See Commuters? A Critical Benchmark of Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00661)
*Ashiq Shukoor Iqbal,Wilson Wongso,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较四类卫星视觉编码器在统一 WeDAN 图扩散框架下生成通勤 OD 的表现，发现 RemoteCLIP 在域内最强，AlphaEarth 等地理编码器零样本迁移更稳健，但跨洲全球城市 OD 生成整体仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通勤起讫点（OD）生成依赖人口普查数据，而卫星基础模型可提供全球可用替代方案；但尚无研究在同一下游流程中系统比较不同卫星编码器范式的效果与迁移能力。

Method: 作者在相同的 WeDAN 图扩散管线中，对四种卫星视觉编码器进行消融比较：语言监督 RemoteCLIP、自监督 DINOv3、地理基础 SatCLIP 与 AlphaEarth；实验覆盖 1,925 个美国县、325 个英国地区和 14 个全球城市，并使用五个随机种子评估。

Result: RemoteCLIP 获得最佳域内表现（CPC 0.602）；AlphaEarth 在英国地区零样本迁移中相较 RemoteCLIP 提升 33% CPC；DINOv3 尽管预训练语料更大，但域内比 RemoteCLIP 低 0.091 CPC，全球场景降至 CPC 0.022；全球城市迁移整体效果很差，最佳 RemoteCLIP CPC 仅 0.122。

Conclusion: 语言监督特征更适合域内 OD 生成，地理基础编码器具备更可靠的零样本迁移性，但仅扩大预训练数据规模不足以保证下游性能；跨洲 OD 生成仍是开放难题，同时论文强调需正确理解 census noise 参数 η 在跨洲评估中的语义变化。

Abstract: Satellite foundation models offer a globally available alternative to census data for commuting origin-destination (OD) generation, yet no study has systematically compared encoder paradigms within a single downstream pipeline. We ablate four satellite vision encoders: language-supervised (RemoteCLIP), self-supervised (DINOv3), and geographically grounded (SatCLIP, AlphaEarth) within an identical WeDAN graph diffusion framework across 1,925 US counties, 325 UK districts, and 14 global cities under five random seeds. Three main findings emerge. First, language-supervised features achieve the strongest in-distribution performance (RemoteCLIP CPC 0.602), while geographically grounded encoders transfer more reliably zero-shot: AlphaEarth improves CPC by 33% over RemoteCLIP on UK districts. Second, pretraining corpus scale alone is insufficient: DINOv3, trained on a substantially larger satellite corpus, underperforms RemoteCLIP by 0.091 CPC in-distribution and collapses to CPC 0.022 globally. Third, no encoder transfers usefully to global cities (best CPC 0.122 for RemoteCLIP, 0.022 for DINOv3), confirming cross-continental OD generation remains an open problem. We additionally clarify the semantics of the census noise parameter $η$, whose ordering reverses under cross-continental evaluation, a distinction critical to correctly interpreting prior results. Training scripts and evaluation logs will be released.

</details>


### [26] [Separating perception from reasoning in vision-language models: a model-free render ceiling for crystal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00663)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需第二模型参与的“render ceiling”评测参照，用于区分视觉语言模型在图像读取与推理环节的误差，并在渲染晶体结构基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态评测难以判断视觉语言模型的错误源于误读图像还是基于图像的推理失败，因为区分这两类错误通常需要引入另一个模型，从而带来额外偏差。

Method: 作者针对由已知对象渲染生成的基准，提出模型无关的render ceiling：通过反演冻结相机参数并重新求解跨视角对应关系，恢复图像所能支持的精确答案；同时证明该上界只会因可枚举的投影重合情况失效，并在2,160个渲染晶体结构上验证该失效集合为空。

Result: 在14个视觉语言模型上，提供精确几何文本信息能提升所有模型表现，但13个模型的差距缩小不到一半；一个无语言组件的监督视觉模型在相同图像读取任务上达到0.8952，超过所有视觉语言模型。

Conclusion: 该方法能够把模型缺陷更精确地归因于模型自身，揭示被下游准确率误判为推理错误的提取阶段虚构问题，并为基准构建中的相机放置提供规则；方法可迁移到任何具有可逆前向渲染过程的基准。

Abstract: Multimodal evaluations cannot say whether a vision-language model misread an image or misreasoned about it, because every existing method for separating the two places a second model in the loop. We introduce the render ceiling, a model-free reference for benchmarks built by rendering known objects: inverting the frozen cameras and re-solving cross-view correspondence recovers exactly the answer the images support. We prove the ceiling fails only through an enumerable set of projection coincidences and certify that set empty on 2,160 rendered crystal structures, so every point of a model's deficit belongs to the model. Across fourteen vision-language models, supplying exact geometry as text lifts every model yet closes under half the gap for thirteen, while a supervised vision model with no language component reads the same images at 0.8952, above every vision-language model. The instrument exposes extraction-stage fabrication that downstream accuracy would misattribute to reasoning, yields camera-placement rules for benchmark builders, and transfers to any benchmark with an invertible forward rendering.

</details>


### [27] [DGNet: Dual-knowledge Guided Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00666)
*Chenglong Yu,Mingzhu Xu,Jing Wang,Tongtong Wang,Pingping Miao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DGNet用于红外小目标检测，通过多种可泛化文本知识引导网络，在不依赖图像特定提示和推理期外部模型的情况下提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导IRSTD方法存在两点不足：单一文本同时描述背景与目标会造成语义纠缠，不利于背景抑制和目标增强；依赖图像特定文本提示通常需要推理阶段调用CLIP等外部模型，限制部署。

Method: 提出Dual-knowledge Guided Network（DGNet）。其中，Prior-knowledge Wavelet Modulation（PWM）模块利用分别表征大尺度背景和稀疏目标的双文本先验，在频域中解耦并调制纠缠语义；Consensus-knowledge Directional Alignment（CDA）损失将跨样本初始状态与理想目标分别建模为“复杂背景”和“明亮目标”，为模型构建统一明确的优化方向。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，DGNet取得了优于现有方法的检测性能，并验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: DGNet通过解耦背景与目标文本知识、引入频域调制和方向一致性优化，有效缓解语义纠缠与部署依赖问题，是一种更具泛化性和实用性的文本引导红外小目标检测方法。

Abstract: InfRared Small Target Detection (IRSTD) is a prominent and challenging task in computer vision. In recent years, text-guided methods have significantly improved detection performance. However, they still suffer from two key limitations. First, a single text description simultaneously modeling both background and target leads to semantic entanglement, which contradicts the objective of background suppression and target enhancement. Second, reliance on image-specific textual prompts (requiring additional external models such as CLIP during inference) results in deployment constraints. To address these issues, we propose a novel Dual-knowledge Guided Network (DGNet) based on multiple generalizable texts. Specifically, we design a Prior-knowledge Wavelet Modulation (PWM) module, which leverages dual textual priors that separately characterize large-scale backgrounds and sparse targets to effectively disentangle and modulate entangled semantics in the frequency domain. Furthermore, we introduce a Consensus-knowledge Directional Alignment (CDA) loss, which models the initial state and the ideal target across samples as `complex background' and `bright target', respectively, thereby constructing a clear and unified directional optimization trajectory for the model. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate the superior performance of DGNet and the effectiveness of each component. The source code is available at https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/MM26-DGNet.

</details>


### [28] [FTU-Seek: Foundation Model-Guided Hard-Negative Learning for Sparse Functional Tissue Unit Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00704)
*Zonghao Liu,Lei Su,Jiguang Yu,Xuqing Geng,Louis Shuo Wang,Jianmin Wang,Jingfeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FTU-Seek利用病理基础模型特征和形态感知的困难阴性样本选择，提升稀疏功能性组织单元在全切片图像中的分割训练效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性组织单元（如TLS、血管、腺体）对组织结构和疾病相关组织形态研究很重要，但它们在WSI中通常稀疏、异质，并被大量形态相似背景包围，导致自动分割困难。

Method: 框架使用冻结的UNI病理基础模型多层特征训练补丁级分类器，区分含目标与不含目标组织；随后按目标存在概率对阴性补丁排序，并用静态TopK策略选取高分困难阴性样本，构建紧凑的分割训练集。

Result: 研究通过五折交叉验证、内部测试队列以及TLS的独立30张WSI保留队列评估，并与positive-only、all-tissue、random-negative和matched random TopK等采样策略比较；还在外部TCGA队列探索分割衍生表型。

Conclusion: 该摘要表明，形态感知的困难阴性补丁选择是稀疏FTU分割中的关键环节，FTU-Seek有望在减少训练冗余的同时提高多类组织结构分割的稳健性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Functional tissue units (FTUs), including tertiary lymphoid structures (TLSs), blood vessels, and glands, encode localized immune, vascular, and epithelial organization in histopathology. Accurate quantification of these structures is important for studying tissue architecture and disease-associated tissue organization. However, FTUs are frequently sparse, heterogeneous, and surrounded by large amounts of morphologically similar background tissue, making automated segmentation in whole-slide images (WSIs) challenging. We therefore developed FTU-Seek, a pathology foundation model-guided framework that treats morphology-aware negative-patch selection as a key component of sparse FTU segmentation. FTU-Seek uses frozen multi-depth features from the UNI pathology foundation model to train a patch-level classifier that distinguishes FTU-containing from FTU-absent tissue. Target-absent patches are subsequently ranked according to their predicted target-containing probabilities, and the highest-scoring hard negatives are selected through a static Top$K$ strategy to construct compact segmentation training sets. The framework was evaluated using five-fold cross-validation and internal test cohorts across TLS, blood-vessel, and gland segmentation tasks, with an additional independent 30-WSI held-out cohort for TLS. Positive-only, all-tissue, random-negative, and matched random Top$K$ sampling strategies served as comparators. Segmentation-derived phenotypes were further explored in external TCGA cohorts.

</details>


### [29] [Controllable Image Captioning with Prompt-Conditioned Scene Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00709)
*Jongyeop Hyun,Taeyoung Kim,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoCUS 通过基于场景图组件奖励的可控优化，使视觉语言模型能按自然语言控制提示强调或避免特定语义内容，从而提升细粒度图像描述的可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型能够生成流畅的图像描述，但缺乏可靠的语义控制能力；用户难以指定描述应突出属性、关系或特定图像区域，也难以抑制不希望出现的内容。

Method: 提出 FoCUS：将生成的 caption 解析并对齐到场景图组件（对象、属性、关系等），根据用户控制提示为不同组件分配正/负权重，形成 prompt-conditioned 控制目标；使用 GRPO 优化该目标，并通过更严格的对象有效性阈值以及基于推理的属性/关系验证提升评分可靠性。还提出 SCoPE 基准，使用对比式 Include/Avoid 约束评估目标覆盖率与越界内容抑制能力。

Result: 在两个 VLM backbone 上的实验表明，FoCUS 持续提升可控性和细粒度描述质量，同时不损害通用图像描述性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，将自然语言控制提示与场景图对齐奖励结合，并通过强化学习优化，可以有效增强图像描述中的细粒度语义控制能力；SCoPE 也为后续可控 captioning 研究提供了更针对性的评估框架。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models produce fluent image descriptions but offer limited semantic control: users cannot reliably specify whether captions should emphasize attributes, relations, or particular image regions. We present Fine-grained Captioning Control Using Scene Rewards (FoCUS), a controllable image captioning method that lets users steer captions toward specific semantic emphases through natural-language control prompts. The core idea is a prompt-conditioned control objective based on scene-graph-aligned component scores. Generated captions are parsed and aligned to scene-graph components such as objects, attributes, and relations. These components are differentially weighted, including negative weights, according to the requested emphasis. We optimize this objective with GRPO and further improve its reliability through a stricter object validity threshold and reasoning-based verification for attribute and relation scoring. To evaluate controllability, we introduce Semantic Control and Precision Evaluation (SCoPE), a benchmark with contrastive Include/Avoid constraints for measuring both target content coverage and out-of-scope suppression. Experiments on two VLM backbones show that FoCUS consistently improves controllability and fine-grained caption quality without degrading general caption performance.

</details>


### [30] [EarthLD: Towards Unified Open-World Landslide Understanding via Vision-Language Guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00712)
*Yuanchao Su,Lianru Gao,Mengying Jiang,Jiangyi Chen,Jiaxin Cheng,Yicong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EarthLD，一个视觉-语言引导的扩散框架，用于遥感影像中的开放世界滑坡识别、制图与触发因素解释，并在跨区域、跨传感器实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑坡是常见地质灾害，但其形态不规则、光谱特征模糊，且不同遥感平台间存在显著域偏移，导致自动检测与制图仍具挑战。研究旨在构建更统一、鲁棒且可泛化的滑坡理解方法。

Method: 作者将滑坡理解建模为扩散过程，从噪声潜在表示中逐步推断滑坡的存在性、空间范围与像素级边界；在去噪过程中融合视觉观测与上下文知识，实现图像级识别、像素级制图和不确定性表征。同时构建了全球尺度开放世界滑坡基准数据集，整合多个公开遥感数据源。

Result: 在不同区域、传感器和触发事件条件下的大量实验表明，EarthLD 在滑坡检测任务上持续优于现有方法，并能输出带置信度的疑似区域预测与滑坡范围图。

Conclusion: EarthLD 为全球地质灾害监测与应急响应提供了一种统一、稳健的解决方案，尤其适用于开放世界场景下的滑坡识别、制图和解释任务。

Abstract: Landslides are widespread geological hazards, yet their automated detection and mapping in remote sensing imagery remain challenging because of their irregular morphology, ambiguous spectral signatures, and substantial domain shifts across imaging platforms. To overcome these challenges, we propose EarthLD, a vision-language-guided diffusion framework for open-world landslide understanding, enabling unified landslide recognition, mapping, and trigger interpretation. At its core, EarthLD formulates landslide understanding as a diffusion process that progressively infers the presence, spatial extent, and pixel-level boundaries of landslides from noisy latent representations. This probabilistic formulation enables the model to jointly perform image-level landslide recognition and mapping while characterizing predictive uncertainty. By integrating visual observations with contextual knowledge in the denoising process, EarthLD distinguishes diverse landslides from backgrounds, produces confidence-aware predictions for suspected regions, and maps landslide ranges. We additionally construct a global-scale open-world landslide benchmark by systematically harmonizing multiple publicly available remote sensing data collected by diverse institutions. Extensive experiments across regions, sensors, and triggering events demonstrate that EarthLD consistently outperforms existing landslide detection methods, highlighting its potential as a unified and robust solution for global geological-hazard monitoring and emergency response.

</details>


### [31] [Efficient and Robust Absolute Pose Estimation via Gravity-Prior-Driven Transformation Decoupling and Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00713)
*Hu Cao,Qianyi Yang,Xinyi Li,Jiong Liu,Yinlong Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用重力方向先验的绝对位姿估计方法，将原始6自由度问题解耦为4自由度，并通过一维全局投票、线性平移求解和位姿精化提升鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对位姿估计是机器人应用中的关键任务。虽然重力方向先验可降低问题复杂度，但在存在大量误匹配时，如何高效、鲁棒地估计位姿，以及如何利用内点对应进一步获得高精度位姿，仍是未充分解决的问题。

Method: 作者提出新的变换策略，利用重力先验导出的几何关系进行变换解耦，将6DoF位姿估计简化为1DoF旋转角与3DoF平移的4DoF问题。旋转角通过一维全局投票获得最优估计；得到旋转后先初步剔除误匹配，再将平移作为线性问题求解；最后引入新的位姿精化算法联合提升旋转和平移精度。

Result: 在合成数据和TUM RGB-D、ETH3D、RobotCar三个真实公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有SOTA方法。集成到ORB-SLAM2后，在KITTI数据集上可减少漂移并改善重定位时的轨迹对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用重力方向先验进行合理的几何解耦，可以显著提升绝对位姿估计的效率、鲁棒性和精度，并对视觉SLAM系统具有实际增益。

Abstract: Estimation of the absolute pose of an object is an essential task for various robotic applications. Recently, incorporating gravity direction as prior information has emerged as a popular approach to simplify absolute pose estimation. However, developing a robust and efficient algorithm to solve this challenging problem remains a difficult question due to large amounts of mismatches. In addition, obtaining an accurate pose solution from selected inlier correspondences with gravity prior is still a research gap. In this paper, we propose a novel transformation strategy that exploits geometric relations derived from the gravity prior. Through transformation decoupling, the original 6 degrees of freedom (DoF) absolute pose estimation problem is simplified into a 4-DoFs problem: 1-DoF for the rotation angle and 3-DoFs for translation, significantly improving the efficiency. For the 1-DoF rotation angle, we apply a one-dimensional global voting algorithm for optimal estimation. Once the optimal rotation is obtained, the mismatched correspondences are preliminarily filtered, and translation estimation, a linear problem, can be easily solved. Furthermore, to obtain accurate pose results, we introduce a novel pose refinement algorithm to enhance the accuracy of both rotation and translation. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and three publicly available real-world datasets (TUM RGB-D, ETH3D, and RobotCar) demonstrate that the proposed method achieves stronger performance compared to existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. To further validate our method, we integrated it into ORB-SLAM2. The results on the KITTI dataset show it effectively reduces drift and improves trajectory alignment during relocalization. The source code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [32] [Design and Implementation of a Kalman Filter-Infused Algorithm for Tilt Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00730)
*Yuehan Ma,Hongji Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于MPU6050与RP2040平台的单轴倾角估计系统，使用卡尔曼滤波融合加速度计与陀螺仪数据，以降低噪声并抑制漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本惯性传感器在机器人、运动跟踪和嵌入式控制等应用中常用于倾角测量，但加速度计易受噪声和短时扰动影响，陀螺仪积分又会产生长期漂移，因此需要一种稳定、实时且适合嵌入式平台的传感器融合方法。

Method: 系统使用MPU6050采集加速度计和陀螺仪数据，并在RP2040微控制器上实时处理。加速度计通过重力方向直接估计倾角，陀螺仪提供角速度并经积分得到角度变化；随后采用卡尔曼滤波融合两类测量，利用加速度计的长期稳定性和陀螺仪的短期平滑性。论文还通过仿真建模传感器噪声和漂移，并进行硬件实验验证。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，与仅使用加速度计或仅使用陀螺仪相比，卡尔曼滤波融合方法能够有效降低测量噪声，抑制长期漂移，同时保持较好的动态响应。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了更稳定、更准确的单轴倾角估计，说明基于卡尔曼滤波的低成本惯性传感器融合方案在嵌入式应用中具有实用性和可实现性。

Abstract: Accurate tilt angle estimation is important in many engineering applications, such as robotics, motion tracking, and embedded control systems. However, measurements from low-cost inertial sensors are often degraded by noise and drift. This paper presents a single-axis tilt angle estimation system based on the MPU6050 inertial measurement unit, implemented on an RP2040 microcontroller platform, with sensor fusion achieved through a Kalman filter. The accelerometer provides a direct estimate of tilt angle from gravity but is sensitive to noise and short-term fluctuations. The gyroscope provides smooth angular rate measurements, but integration over time introduces drift. To overcome these limitations, a Kalman filter is used to combine measurements from both sensors, leveraging the long-term stability of the accelerometer and the short-term smoothness of the gyroscope. Both simulation and hardware experiments are performed. In simulation, sensor noise and drift are modeled to evaluate the filter performance under control conditions. In the hardware implementation, real-time MPU6050 data is acquired and processed by the RP2040 platform, and the estimated tilt angle is compared with accelerometer-only and gyroscope-only outputs. The results show that the proposed method effectively reduces noise measurements and suppresses long-term drift while preserving good dynamic response. Overall, the system provides more stable and accurate tilt estimation than either sensor alone, demonstrating a practical and accessible approach for Kalman filter based sensor fusion in embedded application.

</details>


### [33] [Mind the Rift: Cross-Scale Coupling Mismatch for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00742)
*Siyu Li,Jin Yang,Weiheng Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 RIFT，用宏观语义时序轨迹与微观像素残差之间的跨尺度耦合不一致来检测 AI 生成视频，在多个基准上取得接近 99% 以上的 F1，并对未知生成器与不同编码器具有较强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 视频生成模型达到电影级真实感，传统单尺度伪造检测信号可能不足。作者观察到真实视频中宏观时间动态与微观残差模式由统一成像物理过程共同产生，二者存在内在耦合；而生成模型通常未显式保持这种联合分布，因此会产生跨尺度耦合失配，可作为新的取证信号。

Method: 提出 RIFT 框架，包括三部分：宏观流利用学习到的流形轨迹、微分几何与持久同调建模预期时间演化；微观流使用隐写分析滤波与时间建模捕获像素级残差取证线索；耦合散度模块度量两路表示之间的条件依赖关系，并通过 Gram-Schmidt 正交化保证该度量的信息论有效性。

Result: 在 VidProM（12 万视频、7 个生成器）和 GenVidBench（6.8 万视频、4 个生成器）上分别达到 99.33% 与 99.72% F1；留一生成器评估中未知生成器检测率为 97.87%。模型对编码器较不敏感：ViT-S/14 到 ViT-L/14 的 F1 变化小于 0.1%，切换到 DINOv1 仅下降 0.73 个百分点。

Conclusion: 跨尺度耦合失配是一个有潜力且稳健的 AI 视频检测信号。RIFT 通过分离建模宏观动态、微观残差并量化二者关系，在高性能、未知生成器泛化和编码器无关性方面表现突出，但仍需关注其计算复杂度、真实场景压缩/后处理鲁棒性以及对更多生成模型和数据分布的验证。

Abstract: As AI video generators achieve cinematic realism, reliable detection becomes essential for safeguarding digital trust. We identify cross-scale coupling mismatch as a new forensic signal, where scale refers to the level of abstraction (semantic dynamics vs. pixel-level residuals): in natural videos, macro-level temporal dynamics and micro-level residual patterns are intrinsically coupled by the unified imaging physics pipeline, whereas AI generators, whose training objectives do not explicitly preserve this joint distribution, systematically violate this coupling. Detecting such mismatch is challenging because it requires independently extracting information at both scales while simultaneously quantifying their cross-scale relationship. We propose RIFT (Representation Inconsistency Forensics on Trajectories), an orthogonal forensic framework that addresses this through three interlocking components: a macro stream that builds a dynamic baseline of expected temporal evolution via differential geometry and persistent homology on learned manifold trajectories, a micro stream that acts as a sensitive forensic probe via steganalytic filtering and temporal modeling, and a coupling divergence module that measures the conditional dependency between the two streams. Gram-Schmidt orthogonality guarantees the information-theoretic validity of this measurement. Experiments on two benchmarks (VidProM, 120K videos, 7 generators; GenVidBench, 68K videos, 4 generators) demonstrate that RIFT achieves 99.33% and 99.72% F1-score respectively, with 97.87% unseen-generator detection rate in leave-one-out evaluation, while exhibiting encoder agnosticism: scaling from ViT-S/14 (22M) to ViT-L/14 (300M) changes F1 by less than 0.1%, and switching to a different encoder family (DINOv1) reduces F1 by only 0.73 pp. Code is available at https://github.com/Litsay/RIFT

</details>


### [34] [Feed-Forward Multi-view Multi-person Reconstruction with Contrastive Human-Aware 3D Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00745)
*Yuanwang Yang,Buzhen Huang,Zongxuan Ren,Jing Huang,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种自顶向下的多视角多人三维人体重建框架，在统一的实例中心三维空间中同时处理相机标定、跨视角关联与人体重建，并通过空间对比学习提升遮挡场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角人体重建多在简化条件下有效；底层自底向上方法通常依赖精确相机标定和显式跨视角匹配，在无约束环境、多人场景、严重遮挡和身份歧义下性能受限。

Method: 构建统一的、以人体实例为中心的人体感知三维空间，将多视角观测提升并融合到共享3D空间中，联合编码几何结构、视觉外观和人体语义线索；引入空间对比学习，使不同视角/模态中同一人体实例的3D特征对齐，同时拉开不同实例；最后从实例级3D人体token前馈回归SMPL参数。

Result: 实验表明该方法在挑战性真实场景中实现了更鲁棒、准确且高效的多视角多人三维人体重建，尤其提升了跨视角一致性和遮挡条件下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将跨视角匹配、语义聚合、实例区分和人体参数回归统一到原生3D空间中，提供了面向复杂真实环境的多视角多人重建新范式。

Abstract: Multi-view human reconstruction has been extensively studied under simplified settings, yet robust and efficient multi-person reconstruction in unconstrained environments remains challenging. Existing bottom-up methods often rely on accurate camera calibration and explicit cross-view matching, and therefore struggle with severe occlusions and ambiguities. We propose a new top-down paradigm that maintains a unified, instance-centric human-aware 3D space, enabling simultaneous camera calibration, cross-view association, and human reconstruction via cross-modal contrastive learning. Observations from multiple views are lifted and fused into this shared 3D space, where geometric structure, visual appearance, and human-centric semantic cues are jointly encoded at the instance level. We further introduce a spatial contrastive learning strategy that aligns 3D features corresponding to the same human instance across different views and modalities while separating different instances. This enables correspondence reasoning, semantic aggregation, and instance discrimination to be performed natively in 3D, improving cross-view consistency and robustness under severe occlusions.
