Across sparse-reward visual reasoning and navigation tasks, SAGE learns policies that act without VLM guidance at evaluation time and improves over unguided RL in several environments, including settings where the learned policy exceeds its VLM teacher. The results show that selective guidance is most beneficial when the VLM can help the agent discover high-reward trajectories, and less useful when unguided exploration already succeeds or teacher actions do not lead to informative experience. SAGE also reduces VLM usage by prompting the teacher only on a fraction of training steps and requiring no VLM calls at deployment. Overall, our results suggest that VLMs don't need to be used as fixed policies to be useful; they can instead act as temporary, imperfect sources of guidance whose value is tested and internalized through interaction.

</details>


### [355] [MiNER: Fine-Tuned Biomedical Natural Language Processing for Malaria Disease Entity Recognition in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00073)
*V. S. Anoop,Devika N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微调 BioBERT 的方法，用于从疟疾相关科学文献中抽取生物医学实体与关系，并发布人工标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾相关文献数量庞大且持续增长，研究者需要高效抽取关键生物医学信息，以支持分子机制、流行病学与治疗干预研究；传统信息抽取方法难以充分利用上下文语义。

Method: 构建并预处理疟疾科学文献语料，对具有临床意义的实体进行人工标注；采用预训练生物医学语言模型 BioBERT 编码文本，利用领域标注数据进行监督微调，以提升命名实体及关系抽取能力；与多种编码方法和机器学习算法进行实验比较。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在精确率、召回率和准确率等指标上显著优于对比方法；同时发布了人工标注的实体与关系抽取数据集。

Conclusion: 微调后的 BioBERT 能有效提升疟疾文献中的生物医学信息抽取性能，所发布数据集也有助于推动相关健康信息学研究。

Abstract: Malaria remains a significant global health burden, necessitating continuous research efforts to understand its complex molecular mechanisms, epidemiology, and potential therapeutic interventions. Extracting essential biomedical information from the vast and constantly growing malaria literature is a challenging task that demands innovative approaches. Recently, pre-trained language models have revolutionized natural language processing tasks, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in various domains. This paper proposes a fine-tuned pre-trained biomedical language model for biomedical information extraction from scientific literature on malaria disease. The proposed methodology selects and preprocesses a large corpus of scientific articles on malaria, and then annotates them with entities of clinical significance. It then leverages BioBERT, a state-of-the-art pre-trained language model, to encode the textual data into context-aware representations. We fine-tune the model using domain-specific annotations and supervised learning to enhance its ability to extract relevant biomedical named entities. Extensive experiments and comparisons with different encoding and machine learning algorithms show that the proposed approach significantly outperforms them in precision, recall, and accuracy. We also publish our human-labeled dataset for entity and relation extraction to enable other health informatics researchers to train advanced models for malaria information extraction.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [356] [Capacity Achieving Torn Paper Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00522)
*Junsheng Liu,Netanel Raviv*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多级连续局部对齐与导频回收方案，在撕纸信道中以较小导频开销逐步恢复更短片段，并以高概率达到信道容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有随机码虽可达容量但解码复杂度指数级；Shomorony 和 Vahid 的交织导频方案依赖全局统计唯一性，只能对齐足够长的片段；Liu 和 Raviv 的局部对齐方案降低了可对齐片段长度要求，但固定导频序列需要同时兼顾导频长度与短片段对齐能力，存在设计权衡与限制。

Method: 用多级 successive local alignment 与 pilot-recycling 替代固定导频序列。第一层使用导频序列和独立随机线性码对齐并解码最长片段；随后将已恢复的信息作为更大的导频序列用于下一层，重复该过程以逐级恢复更短片段。构造依赖一系列随机线性码的选择。

Result: 证明对任意 ε>0，存在一组随机线性码选择，使得当块长趋于无穷时，该方案以高概率达到至少低于撕纸信道容量 ε 的码率。

Conclusion: 通过导频回收和多级局部对齐，本文消除了固定导频设计中的关键限制，在保持较小导频长度的同时逐步对齐更短碎片，从而实现撕纸信道容量。

Abstract: In the torn paper channel, a codeword is cut at random locations, and the resulting error-free fragments are delivered to the decoder as an unordered multiset. Although the capacity of this channel can be achieved using random code, decoding such codes generally requires exponential time. The interleaved-pilot construction of Shomorony and Vahid embeds a De Bruijn sequence among the symbols of a shifted erasure code and aligns only fragments that are sufficiently long through global statistical uniqueness. A subsequent local-alignment scheme by Liu and Raviv employs run-length-limited constraints and exclusive all-zero markers to identify pilot positions from local structure, substantially reducing the minimum fragment length that can be aligned.
  We further improve the method of Liu and Raviv by replacing its fixed pilot sequence with a multilevel successive local alignment and pilot-recycling procedure. In Liu and Raviv, the pilot sequence is chosen once and must simultaneously balance the length of the pilot sequence against the ability to align short fragments. Our construction removes this limitation by reusing pilot sequences across successive decoding levels. A pilot sequence with an independent random linear code is first used to align and decode the longest fragments. The information recovered at this stage is then recycled as a larger pilot sequence for the next stage.This process is repeated over multiple levels, so that progressively shorter fragments are recovered while the length of the pilot sequence remains small. Our construction depends on choosing a series of random linear codes, and we show that for any~$\varepsilon>0$, there exists a choice of such codes which attains rate of at least~$\varepsilon$ below the capacity, with high probability as the block length goes to infinity. Therefore, our construction achieves the capacity of the torn-paper channel.

</details>


### [357] [Solutions to Three Conjectures and an Open Problem on Binary BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00532)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Jiawei He,Boru Yi,Dabin Zheng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二元 BCH 码若干未解决的最小距离问题，证明三个猜想并部分解决一个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCH 码是重要的循环码，其精确最小距离直接决定纠错能力；现有 BCH 界仅给出下界，许多参数族的精确最小距离仍难确定。

Method: 针对 Chen、Xie 和 Ding 提出的三个猜想及一个开放问题，作者通过构造达到 BCH 界的码字来证明若干二元 BCH 码族的最小距离；并进一步分析更困难的码族，在特定情形下给出最小距离或充分条件，并用反例否定一般性断言。

Result: 三个关于二元 BCH 码精确最小距离的猜想被解决；另一个更困难码族在部分情形下确定了最小距离；开放问题 8.4 中前两个长度族得到肯定回答，第三个长度族给出充分条件，同时发现无约束命题一般不成立。

Conclusion: 论文推进了二元 BCH 码最小距离理论，说明通过显式构造达到 BCH 界的码字可精确刻画多个参数族，但某些开放断言需要附加条件，不能直接推广到所有情形。

Abstract: BCH codes are among the most important classes of cyclic codes and have played a central role in coding theory and its applications. One of the fundamental problems in the study of BCH codes is to determine their exact minimum distances, which directly govern their error-correcting capability. Although the BCH bound provides a general lower bound, determining the exact minimum distance is often difficult, and many parameter families remain unresolved. In this paper, we investigate three conjectures and an open problem on binary BCH codes proposed by Chen, Xie, and Ding in \cite{Chen59}. We settle these conjectures on the exact minimum distances of three families of binary BCH codes by constructing codewords attaining the BCH bound. Beyond these conjectures, we further study the more difficult family codes and determine its minimum distance for some cases. We further study Open Problem 8.4: affirmative answers are obtained for the first two length families, while for the third family a sufficient condition is established and a counterexample shows that the unrestricted assertion does not hold in general.

</details>


### [358] [MaskCode: Mask Transformer for Feedback-Assisted Coding With Linear Block Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00715)
*Jonggyu Jang,Hongjae Nam,Vishrant Tripathi,David J. Love,Hyun Jong Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出 MaskCode，一种面向级联编码系统的 Transformer 内反馈码，通过显式利用外层线性分组码结构，在 BCH 和 LDPC 外码上取得优于基线的性能，最高约 1.5 dB SNR 增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的反馈编码方案通常假设完美反馈或未充分利用外层纠错码结构，可能把反馈资源浪费在外层 ECC 已能纠正的错误模式上，限制了级联反馈编码系统的效率与性能。

Method: 提出 Transformer-based 内反馈码 MaskCode，并通过两种机制引入外层线性分组码结构知识：其一，使用软 syndrome 输入提示潜在的校验约束违背；其二，根据 Tanner 图构造 code-aware attention mask，引导注意力机制关注与码结构相关的信息。此外，作者比较了端到端训练中可微 BP 解码器的作用。

Result: 在 BCH 和 LDPC 外码上的大量实验表明，MaskCode 持续优于所有基线方法，最高获得 1.5 dB 的信噪比增益。实验还发现，通过可微 BP 解码器进行端到端训练并未带来额外收益，反而因迭代 BP 中的梯度爆炸导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 显式建模外层码结构能够显著提升 ML-based 反馈编码在级联系统中的资源分配与纠错性能；MaskCode 的结构感知设计已有效内化外码约束，因此无需依赖可微 BP 解码器进行端到端联合优化。

Abstract: Feedback-based coding schemes have demonstrated substantial performance gains over today's open-loop coding schemes. Unfortunately, these gains are usually achieved in idealized settings with perfect feedback. Over the last few years, machine learning-based schemes have been shown to be promising solutions for implementing feedback-based codes, particularly when combined with short-block-length open-loop error correcting codes (ECCs) in a concatenated coding structure. However, existing ML-based feedback schemes remain agnostic to the outer code's structure, potentially misallocating feedback resources on error patterns already correctable by the outer ECC. To address this, we propose MaskCode, a Transformer-based inner feedback code for concatenated coding systems, which explicitly incorporates structural knowledge of the outer linear block code into the inner feedback encoder design via two synergistic mechanisms: 1) a soft syndrome-based input that informs the encoder about potential parity constraint violations, and 2) a code-aware attention mask derived from the Tanner graph. We further show that end-to-end training with a differentiable belief propagation (BP) decoder offers no additional gain, as MaskCode's structure-aware design already internalizes the structural knowledge of the outer code; in fact, backpropagation through the iterative BP decoder introduces gradient explosion, which degrades rather than improves performance. Extensive evaluations on BCH and LDPC outer codes demonstrate that MaskCode consistently outperforms all baselines, achieving up to 1.5 dB SNR gain.

</details>


### [359] [Coding for Multiple Reverse-Complement and Palindromic Duplications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00779)
*Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究反向互补（RC）与回文（PAL）重复错误下的 q 元纠错码，给出多错误情形的冗余下界、偶数 k 时 RC 与 PAL 的等价变换、两次错误后代数量极值，以及基于图着色的存在性码构造界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于 RC/PAL 重复错误纠正的理论仍不完整，尤其是顺序发生的多次重复错误、偶数长度 k 的 RC 情形，以及两次错误时码冗余上下界之间的关系需要进一步刻画。

Method: 作者建立信息论型冗余下界；针对偶数 k 构造逐坐标双射，将 RC 重复转化为 PAL 重复；分析恰好两次错误产生的不同后代数；再构造以共同 exact-two 后代为邻接关系的图，通过度数估计与贪心染色证明纠错码的存在性。

Result: 固定 q,k,t 时任意长度 n 的 t 错误纠正码冗余至少为 t log_q n - O(1)；当 t=o(n) 时下界为 t log_q(n/t)-O(t)。偶数 k 下 RC 与 PAL 码等价且大小不变。两次错误的最大后代数由交替两符号词达到（PAL），RC 情形由双射得到。图着色给出冗余 4 log_q n+O(1) 的存在性码。

Conclusion: 本文统一并扩展了 RC/PAL 重复错误纠错码的理论，特别解决了偶数 k 下 RC 与 PAL 的等价问题，并给出两错误情形的新存在性上界；但二元两错误问题中仍存在下界 2 log_2 n 与上界 4 log_2 n 之间的因子二差距。

Abstract: Reverse-complement (RC) and palindromic (PAL) duplications copy a length-$k$ block, reverse the copy, and insert it next to the original block; an RC duplication also complements the copied symbols. We study $q$-ary codes correcting $t$ such operations performed sequentially, so a later operation may copy symbols created by an earlier one. For fixed $q,k,t$, every length-$n$ code $C$ for either channel satisfies $n-\log_q|C|\geq t\log_q n-O_{q,k,t}(1)$; for fixed $q,k$ and $t=o(n)$ the lower bound is $t\log_q(n/t)-O_{q,k}(t)$. For a single RC error over an even alphabet with a fixed-point-free complement, the previously known RC-specific lift applies at odd $k$ and does not cover even $k$. For every even $k$, we give a coordinate-wise bijection that turns each RC duplication into a PAL duplication. Applying this bijection to every codeword therefore converts any $t$-error-correcting RC code into a PAL code of the same size, and conversely; encoders and decoders transfer by adding linear-time coordinate passes. We also determine the maximum number of distinct descendants produced by exactly two errors from one source word. Words alternating between any two distinct alphabet symbols attain this maximum for PAL, and the bijection gives the RC maximizers. For both PAL and RC at even $k$, form a graph whose vertices are all $q$-ary words of length $n$, joining two distinct vertices exactly when they have a common exact-two descendant. Bounding the degree and greedily coloring this graph yields existential codes of redundancy $4\log_q n+O_{q,k}(1)$. In the binary two-error problem, the converse gives $2\log_2 n-O_k(1)$, leaving a factor-two gap in the best existence bounds.

</details>


### [360] [Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design of a Lightweight PCG Equalizer with a Fixed Step Size for Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00890)
*Junshuo Wang,Shuting Liu,Shihan Wang,Yan Tian,Jienan Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向硬件实现的粗量化大规模 MIMO 剪切信号恢复/检测的一步校正方法，以显著降低 BEM 中矩阵求逆和动态步长计算的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗量化可降低大规模 MIMO 接收机功耗，但会引入剪切失真；BEM 虽能恢复剪切信号，但矩阵求逆与动态步长评估成为硬件实现瓶颈，因此需要低复杂度、硬件友好的替代方案。

Method: 作者采用初始 Jacobi 预条件共轭梯度方向，并配合固定松弛参数形成一步校正更新；在符号级实现上构建 O(U) 的轻量级前馈数据通路，并进行有限维理论分析。

Result: 该方法在评估的大规模 MIMO 场景中性能接近高分辨率参考检测器；理论上给出精确的一步下降律，证明 Jacobi 归一化可抵消原始近远效应中的乘性缩放，并将加载系统依赖限制在有界衰减因子内；同时给出基于归一化信道相干性的固定步长下降充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过固定步长的一步 PCG/Jacobi 校正，可以在保持接近参考检测性能的同时显著降低硬件复杂度和潜在功耗，适合能效导向的大规模 MIMO 上行链路接收机设计。

Abstract: Coarse quantization in massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems reduces power but causes clipping distortions. The Bayesian Expectation-Maximization (BEM) algorithm can recover clipped signals, but its matrix inversion and dynamic step-size evaluation are hardware bottlenecks. We propose a hardware-friendly one-step correction that uses the initial Jacobi-preconditioned Conjugate Gradient (PCG) direction with a fixed relaxation parameter. The resulting symbol-level update has an ultra-lightweight $\mathcal{O}(U)$ feed-forward datapath and approaches high-resolution reference detectors in the evaluated massive-MIMO setting. Our finite-dimensional analysis establishes the exact one-step descent law, proves that Jacobi normalization cancels the raw multiplicative near-far scaling while confining the loaded-system dependence to bounded attenuation factors, and gives verifiable sufficient conditions for fixed-step descent in terms of normalized channel coherence. System-level results indicate projected power savings for energy-efficient massive MIMO uplinks.

</details>


### [361] [Asymptotically Optimal List Size of Random Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01070)
*Chen Yuan,Ruiqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明固定素数幂 q 下，随机线性码在速率 1-H_q(p)-ε 时以高概率可用列表大小 H_q(p)/ε+O_{p,q}(1) 进行 p-列表译码，解决了此前对任意 q 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表译码容量附近的随机线性码所需列表大小是编码理论中的核心问题。已有工作给出下界约为 H_q(p)/ε，并猜想该量级即足够；二元情形已证明精确到加性常数，但 q>2 时仅有较粗的 C_{p,q}/ε 上界。

Method: 论文针对有限域 F_q 上随机线性码，分析在给定译码半径 p 和容量间隙 ε 下，码字落入任意 Hamming 球的数量，建立高概率的列表大小上界。

Result: 对于每个固定素数幂 q、p∈(0,1-1/q) 以及满足 1-H_q(p)-ε>0 的 ε，速率为 1-H_q(p)-ε 的随机线性码以至少 1-q^{-Ω(n)} 的概率是 (p, ceil(H_q(p)/ε)+O_{p,q}(1))-列表可译码。

Conclusion: 该结果对所有素数幂 q 解决了 Guruswami 等人的猜想，并将 q>2 情形的已知上界从 C_{p,q}/ε 改进为最优主项 H_q(p)/ε 加仅依赖于 p,q 的常数项。

Abstract: We prove that for every fixed prime power $q$, every $p\in(0,1-1/q)$, and every $\varepsilon>0$ with $1-H_q(p)-\varepsilon>0$, a random linear code over $\mathbb{F}_q$ of rate $1-H_q(p)-\varepsilon$ is $(p,\,\left\lceil\frac{H_q(p)}{\varepsilon}\right\rceil+O_{p,q}(1))\text{-list-decodable}$ with probability at least $1-q^{-Ω(n)}$. Guruswami, Li, Mosheiff, Resch, Silas, and Wootters showed that, for sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, random linear codes require list size at least $\left\lfloor\frac{H_q(p)}{\varepsilon}+0.99\right\rfloor,$ and conjectured that $\frac{H_q(p)}{\varepsilon}(1+o(1))$ suffices as $\varepsilon\to 0$. This conjecture was previously known for $q=2$, where the upper bound $H_2(p)/\varepsilon+2$ was established. For $q>2$, however, the best known upper bound was $C_{p,q}/\varepsilon$ for a constant $C_{p,q}$ depending on $p$ and $q$. Our result resolves the conjecture for every prime power $q$ and, in fact, establishes the sharper upper bound $\frac{H_q(p)}{\varepsilon}+O_{p,q}(1)$.

</details>


### [362] [Quantum LDPC and High-Rate CSS Codes from Fair-Density Parity-Check Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01181)
*Hessam Mahdavifar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文基于经典 FDPC 码构造量子 LDPC 与高码率 CSS 码，通过结构化稀疏化与超图乘积获得可解析控制的有限长度参数，并给出逻辑算子低权重重数的解析刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限码率 qLDPC 构造往往难以同时提供有限长度下的认证最小距离、稳定子权重控制以及逻辑算子低权重分布的解析信息。本文旨在构造一类兼具高码率、可控距离、低稳定子权重和可分析误差性能的量子码框架。

Method: 作者从经典 fair-density parity-check（FDPC）码出发，引入对 FDPC 校验矩阵的结构化稀疏化，以降低校验权重并保持组合结构与距离保证；随后结合超图乘积构造得到 qLDPC/CSS 码，并利用 FDPC 的解析权重分布推导量子擦除信道下 ML 逻辑块错误率的一阶表达与高阶近似。

Result: 在量子块长 N<10^5 的有限长度范围内，构造得到保证码率约 0.35% 至 25.8%、认证量子距离 12 至 69、稳定子权重 8 至 16 的 qLDPC 码。渐近情况下，qFDPC 码满足 R_Q=1-O(1/log log N)、D=Ω(N^{1/4})，稳定子权重为 O(log N log log N)。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个有限码率 qLDPC 构造框架，不仅能给出有限长度下的认证距离和参数控制，还能解析刻画低权重逻辑算子重数，从而支持量子擦除信道下有限长度性能与误差平台起始区域的估计。

Abstract: We construct quantum LDPC (qLDPC) and high-rate CSS codes from our recently introduced classical fair-density parity-check (FDPC) codes. To this end, we introduce a structured sparsification of FDPC parity-check matrices, which reduces their check weights while preserving the underlying combinatorial structure and distance guarantees. Combined with the hypergraph-product construction, this yields finite-length qLDPC codes with analytically controlled blocklength, dimension, certified distance, and stabilizer weight. For quantum blocklengths $N<10^5$, the constructions introduced here span guaranteed rates from approximately $0.35\%$ to $25.8\%$ and certified quantum distances from $12$ to $69$, with stabilizer weights between $8$ and $16$.
  In the large-blocklength regime, allowing the FDPC order and sparsified check weight to scale moderately with blocklength yields a family of high-rate CSS codes, which we refer to as quantum FDPC (qFDPC) codes, with rate $R_Q$ and minimum distance $D$ satisfying \[ R_Q =
1-O\left(\frac{1}{\log\log N}\right), \ \ D=Ω(N^{1/4}), \] and stabilizer weight $O(\log N\log\log N)$. Finally, the analytically available FDPC weight distribution provides explicit information about the logical operators of the resulting hypergraph-product codes. Over the quantum erasure channel, this structure yields rigorous first-order maximum likelihood (ML) expressions and a higher-order weight-distribution approximation to the ML logical block error probability. This enables estimation of finite-length operating points and the onset of the error-floor regime.
  To the best of our knowledge, beyond surface-code-type constructions, this is the first finite-rate qLDPC framework to provide both finite-length certified minimum-distance information and an analytical characterization of low-weight logical multiplicities.

</details>


### [363] [Lifted-Product QLDPC Codes in the Polynomial Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01305)
*Vahid Nourozi,Mohsen Moradi,Roxana Smarandache,David G. M. Mitchell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有限长度多项式域表述，用于构造提升积量子低密度奇偶校验（QLDPC）码，可从小规模多项式基矩阵系统生成有限长度码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升积QLDPC码在二进制展开后分析较复杂，尤其是CSS正交/对易条件验证不够简洁；作者希望在二进制循环块展开前，用紧凑代数形式描述并验证构造正确性。

Method: 在商环 F₂[D]/(D^L+1) 上建立有限长度多项式域公式，将多项式基矩阵逐项提升为二进制循环矩阵；证明标准循环提升映射与多项式共轭相容，从而在多项式层面分析CSS正交性；并用NASA约束长度7、码率1/2卷积码及3×4多项式校验矩阵示例说明构造。

Result: 所得二进制校验矩阵满足CSS对易约束；数值示例展示了由小型多项式基矩阵生成有限长度提升积QLDPC码的可行性，并在退极化信道上用多种基准译码器评估了部分构造码的有限长度性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为从小型多项式基矩阵构造有限长度提升积QLDPC码提供了结构化、代数化方法，并使CSS正交性验证在二进制展开前即可完成。

Abstract: This paper presents a finite-length polynomial-domain formulation of lifted-product quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes. We formulate the code construction over the quotient ring F 2[D]/(D L + 1), where polynomial base matrices are lifted entrywise to binary circulant blocks. This representation gives a compact algebraic description of the lifted-product parity-check matrices and allows CSS orthogonality to be analyzed before binary expansion. We show that the standard circulant lifting map is compatible with polynomial conjugation, which implies that the resulting binary matrices satisfy the CSS commutation constraint. The construction is illustrated with a constraint length 7, rate 1/2 NASA convolutional code example, and numerical examples are provided from a 3 x 4 polynomial parity-check matrix. The finite-length performance of selected constructed codes is then evaluated over the depolarizing channel using various benchmark decoders. The resulting framework gives a structured method to construct finite-length lifted-product QLDPC codes from small polynomial base matrices.

</details>
